# Notes: findings too unlikely to hit to be debts
- AskUserQuestion gain/loss rule may refuse terse real choices: check it on real questions. Decision: question-reviewer-agent-hook. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- docs-only-rule-text: Kind `docs-only` still passes an edit to rule text in `docs/decisions/*.md` with no round owed, since it is a `.md` under `docs/`. An edit to `CLAUDE.md` or `skills/*/SKILL.md` is refused now: neither is under `docs/` (merge/merge.py `is_doc`, `kind_misfits`; merge/test_merge.py kind_misfits tests). Decision: docs-only-prs-skip-review.
- guard-paths-md-false-alarm: `merge --review-record` counts every file under a guard folder, so a README there (`hooks/README.md`) must say Guard or check `yes`. Some `.md` files there are read by hooks (`hooks/precompact_handoff_prompt.md`), so the check fails safe (merge/merge.py `guard_hits`, review round 1 of lane merge-guard-flag-from-paths). Decision: pr-review-record.
- guard-suite-red-on-unparsed: hooks/test_guard.py is not green when hooks/guard_wsl.py is parsed and unparsed, so the report has no same-engine gold row for the guard. Cause unmeasured: find which case reads the source text. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- hooks/guard_wsl.py plain-node form excludes only `*` and `?`, so a bracket glob passes: `wsl -e node mods/[ab]/x.test.mjs` (first-file false green). Fix: exclude `*?[]` too, with a refusal case. Decision: wsl-allow-list (WSL allow list).
- agent-brief-crash-silent: on internal error the agent brief hook prints context nowhere and writes stderr, which Claude never sees (hooks/agent_brief.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- agent-brief-no-matcher: SubagentStart agent-brief hook has no matcher, so every agent type gets the standing rules (settings.json) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- agent-brief-text-copy-drifts: agent brief rule text copies CLAUDE.md and agents/*.md; a rule change needs a second edit and the test pins the copy (hooks/agent_brief.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- agent-plan-scope-line-general: Plan and claude-code-guide get the general scope line, which names only reviewer and Explore as never-edit (hooks/agent_brief.py)
- alias-skip-misses-case-forms: python wrapper alias skip misses mixed-case and backslash WindowsApps paths; Git Bash prints canonical form, so theoretical (python wrappers) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- ansi-c-escape-not-decoded: escapes inside $'...' such as \x2f stay text, so a protected path spelled with escapes passes frozen and cap checks (hooks/guard_frozen.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- ask-alone-echo-amp-overdeny: a quoted & in a harmless echo tail counts as a command, so a chain with an ask command over-refuses (hooks/guard.py UNSAFE_TEXT)
- ask-alone-empty-segment-refuses: a trailing newline or ; after a harmless tail stops the tail count, so a chain with an ask command over-refuses (hooks/guard.py harmless_commands)
- ask-alone-ps-backslash-refuses: UNSAFE_TEXT reads \" as an escape in PowerShell, so a quoted path ending in a backslash counts as a step and over-refuses (hooks/guard.py)
- ask-alone-sl-chdir-refuses: sl, chdir and Push-Location are not harmless leads, so a chain starting with them over-refuses; owner ruling names only Set-Location (hooks/guard.py harmless_lead) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-25
- ask-alone-tail-args-unchecked: echo and Write-Host tail arguments are not checked, so a PowerShell (...) subexpression tail counts harmless and asks alone (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ask-alone-tail-test-gap: no ask-alone test puts a quoted #, /* or lone apostrophe inside a harmless && echo or ; Write-Host tail (hooks/test_guard.py)
- bare-merge-reads-folder-not-repo: gh pr merge -R other/repo asks or allows by the current folder's merge-tool stamp, not the named repo (hooks/guard_merge.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- bare-merge-skips-host-merge-tool: mcp__github__merge_pull_request merges bare in a merge-tool repo with no bare-merge ask; merge-checks still guards it (hooks/guard.py)
- bare-merge-tool-unloadable: when merge/merge.py cannot load, merge-checks refuses a bare merge before the bare-merge ask reaches the owner (hooks/guard_merge.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- bare-words-shares-env-list: the bare_words search-tool exemption and the env-file rule share ENV_PATTERN_COMMANDS; a tool added for one changes both (hooks/guard_core.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- base-read-midway-untested: no test covers read_default_base returning unknown when a later verify cannot run (hooks/guard_core.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- blind-git-fixtures-unaudited: other blind-command fixtures, such as make_blind_git, are not audited for the PATH shim gap (janitor/test_sweep.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- builder-fstring-path-unread: an f-string path, such as `open(f'tests/x_{n}.py','w')` in a python body, is not read (hooks/guard_frozen.py _py_hit) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- builder-patch-format-unread: `patch tests/x.py < d.diff`, `git checkout --theirs tests/x`, `ruff format tests/` and `black .` are not refused for a builder (hooks/guard.py builder_shell_test_hit) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- builder-path-forms-unread: `Path('tests').joinpath(...)`, string `+` paths, `dd of=`, `sort -o` and `touch` of a test path are not read (hooks/guard_frozen.py _py_paths, _shell_write_hit) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- shell-write-pipe-redirect: `cat x >| F` is not read as a write to F, so the frozen-path guard allows it (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — Claude rarely writes `>|`, and plain `>` is refused. Until: 2026-11-22
- shell-write-sed-w: `sed -n 'w F' x` writes F and is not read as a write (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — a review finding with no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- shell-write-basetemp: `python3 -m pytest --basetemp=F` writes F and is not read as a write (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — a review finding with no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- frozen-sed-pattern-refused: `sed -n '/rm x/p' F` only reads F, yet it is refused as a write to F (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — a false alarm only refuses. Until: 2026-11-22
- frozen-git-rm-cached-refused: `git rm --cached X && cat X` is refused as a write, because the cat segment names X (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — rare, and the refusal is a minor false alarm. Until: 2026-11-21
- cap-basename-false-alarm: a heredoc writing a fixture named settings.json with the cap key asks, because is_settings_file tests the basename only (hooks/guard.py)
- cap-until-field-taught-late: the _agentCapUntil field is taught only in the revoke message after a bad cap write (hooks/config_watch.py, hooks/guard.py)
- cap-write-by-python-unseen: a cap write made by a Python script, or text piped or written to a script and run later, is not asked (hooks/guard_frozen.py)
- cap-write-glued-ampersand: `(W)&` and `W&` (a cap write backgrounded with a glued `&`) allow, while the spaced form denies as a detached launch (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit). Fix: none — Claude rarely backgrounds a cap write, and the spaced form denies. Until: 2026-11-21
- frozen-trim-quoted-backtick: _shell_write_hit trims a trailing backtick inside a quote too, so a write to a file literally named with a trailing ` reads as the bare name (hooks/guard_frozen.py). Fix: none — no protected file name ends in a backtick; the error can only refuse. Until: 2026-11-21
- catalog-mode-detect-flags: hook catalog finds modes only from a "$g" --flag argument; a script registered twice with a positional arg or -x looks like one mode (lint/hook_catalog.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- cite-by-id-skips-merge-subject: the cite-by-id rule does not read a gh pr merge subject; the lane-by-slug rule does (hooks/guard.py)
- cites-lack-gloss-unchecked: some cites of record ids in decisions and debts carry no gloss and no check requires one (decisions, debts)
- cmd-c-quote-count-bash-reader: bash reader treats \" as an escape but cmd does not, so a cmd /c string can end where the guard disagrees (hooks/guard_core.py) Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-22
- debt-decision-link-unchecked: no check confirms that a debt's decision still names its finding (docs/debts/, the decisions' safety-hole sections)
- debts-no-check-reads-parts: no check reads the four parts of a debt file; check_record_slugs checks only the slug length (lint/check_record_slugs.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- discards-output-slow-path: discards_output re-walks the segment once per redirect match; cost unmeasured (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- drops-cite-forms-unchecked: git commit --trailer, a no-space Refs:decisions/a.md token, git merge -m and git tag -m are never cite-checked (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- drops-dot-slash-guard-lint: ./decisions/a.md after Drops-lines passes the guard but the lint's exact match refuses it (hooks/guard.py cited_record_path)
- drops-mixed-paragraph-exempt: guard exempts a last paragraph mixing prose and the Drops-lines trailer; git does not read it as trailers, so the cite stays in history (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- drops-reads-msg-unlike-git: guard refuses a folded Drops-lines trailer and reads a --file=- body last, unlike git; false refusals only (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- drops-stdin-message-unread: a commit message from -F - stdin is never read, so a prose cite there passes (hooks/guard.py cited_record_path) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- drops-whole-token-no-mutant: no mutant removes the whole-token match of the Drops-lines cite; comma and semicolon cases stand in (hooks/mutate_guard.py, hooks/test_guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- dw-bare-stop-row-blind: test_no_stop_row_runs_decision_watch passes a detector that sees only the quoted Stop row form (lint/test_settings_matcher.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- dw-busy-stop-skipped: a Stop that lands while a judge runs is skipped (SKIP busy) and not judged unless a later Stop comes (hooks/decision_watch.py, mods/decision-watch) Fix: none — owner decides (decision decision-watch-mod-limits). Until: 2026-11-24
- dw-log-keeps-model-text: decision-watch log keeps up to 2000 characters of model FLAG text about a diff, which may quote a secret (mods/decision-watch/hooks/register.mjs) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- dw-log-write-failures-silent: a lost decision-watch log write is silent (empty catch), a read error resets the log, and $.fs.write atomicity is unverified (mods/decision-watch) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- dw-notice-lead-space-lost: the decision-watch log folds whitespace, losing leading whitespace of a notice; no notice starts with whitespace today (mods/decision-watch) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- dw-slash-cache-hit-reads-clean: a repeat /dw after a FLAG prints 'nothing to say' like a clean turn; Stops seen is capped at 500 (mods/decision-watch) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- dw-slot-timers-unmeasured: decision-watch slot timers sum to about 168 s, computed not measured; a timer frees the slot but does not kill the child (mods/decision-watch) Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-22
- env-ansi-c-string: a `$'…'` shell string is not decoded, so `sh -c $'pr\x69ntenv'` allows (hooks/guard_env.py _shell_word). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-child-unlisted-flag: a runner flag in neither its value list nor its bool list (a new or rare flag) is read both ways, so `uv run --foo pytest env` and `pnpm exec --foo jest -c env` deny (hooks/guard_env.py _child_starts). Fix: none — the lists come from each tool's help. A flag the help does not name cannot be known. The both-ways reading keeps the deny that a missed value flag would lose. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-runner-child-runner-unread: a runner given the file whose child is another runner is not read for that runner's own child, so `uv run --env-file F npx -c env` allows (hooks/guard_env.py _child_prints). Fix: none — a runner started by a runner is not a form seen in normal work, and only an attacker writes it (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-script-shell-string-forms: a perl or ruby shell string in a form other than `system`, `exec`, `spawn` or `popen` over a string, a backtick pair, `qx` or `%x` is not read: `IO.popen(["env"])`, `open(P, "env|")`, `Open3`, `system("e" . "nv")` (hooks/guard_env.py ENV_SCRIPT_SHELL_STRING). Fix: none — the string forms are the ones Claude writes by mistake, and each other form needs a parser of the language. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-script-stdin-code: a perl, ruby or awk script read from stdin or a heredoc is not read as code (hooks/guard_env.py _script_code). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-script-string-escape: a quote escape inside a script's code string hides the shell string, so `ruby -e 'system("echo \"x\"; env")'` allows (hooks/guard_env.py ENV_SCRIPT_SHELL_STRING). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-sh-c-escaped-separator: a backslash-escaped separator in an unquoted `sh -c` word is not read as a separator, so `sh -c echo\ x\;env` allows (hooks/guard_env.py _shell_word). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-script-file-unread: a script file that prints the environment, run under a runner given the file, is not read (hooks/guard_env.py _child_prints). Fix: none — the guard reads one command, and the script's text is another call's write. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-echo-variable-any-name: `sh -c 'echo $PORT'` under a runner given the file refuses for any variable name, even one the file does not hold (hooks/guard_env.py _segments_print). Fix: none — the guard cannot know which names the file holds, and a name it holds prints a secret. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-parent-expansion-is-code: a double-quoted shell string with an unescaped `$( )` is expanded by the parent, and its output is the child's code, so `uv run --env-file F sh -c "$(cat script)"` is not read (hooks/guard_env.py _shell_string_prints). Fix: none — the output exists only at run time, and a parent that prints the file's values cannot reach them, since the runner loads the file for the child only. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-flat-crash-allows: a runtime crash in _env_flat allows the call (hooks/guard_env.py) Fix: none — owner decides (decision guard-hooks-fail-closed). Until: 2026-11-23
- env-grep-long-abbrev-unpinned: grep long-option abbreviations of --regexp, --expression and --source, such as --regex=, are a safety hole with no pinning case (hooks/guard_env.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- env-guard-interpreter-api-reads: the env guard allows an env file read through an interpreter API, bare with no comment: python `os.system('cat .env')`, node `require('fs').readFileSync('.env')`, node `require('child_process').execSync('cat .env')` (hooks/guard_env.py). Decision: env-file-stays-unread. Fix: none — no incident. Until: 2026-11-22.
- env-guard-prose-blank-edges: the python and node body reader (hooks/guard_env.py `_blank_code_prose`) allows an env read in forms only a deliberate writer types. Three forms: a `//`-leading shell line inside a node template, a python `<<'PY'` block nested in an unquoted heredoc, and a lone CR in a `//` line. Two more: a triple string holding a space that is itself the path, and a no-space triple string alone on its own line that is a path in a call. A node template literal that names an env file in prose still denies, by design: only `//` lines inside a template are blanked. A python `"""` string holding a space and a `$(` or backtick env read is blanked, so `subprocess.run("""export $(cat ENVFILE | xargs)""", shell=True)` allows, where main refused it. A node regex literal is not read. A `//` or `/*` inside a character class hides the rest of the line or body. So does a lone quote that flips a later string's `//` or `/*`. No case has a lone quote in code text such as JSX `<p>Don't</p>`. Decision: env-file-stays-unread. Fix: none — no incident. Until: 2026-11-22.
- env-guard-prose-reader-false-alarms: the prose reader (hooks/guard_env.py `_rewrite_interpreter_bodies`) reads a body as node or python only when the heredoc's command word, or the child a launcher starts, is the interpreter. Prose that names an env file still denies for `x=$(node - <<'EOF'`, `ts-node -`, a flag before the child (`uv run --with x python -`), `docker run ... python3 -`, `kubectl exec` without `--`, and `yarn`. Main denies these too. Also `docker compose -f F exec`, `docker -H h exec`, `docker --context x exec`, `docker-compose exec` and `npx -y node -`. A docker or podman exec flag outside the known tables leaves the launcher unread. The reader treats the command word as the program: `node script.js <<'EOF'`, `python3 -m x`, `python3 -c` and `node -e` read their stdin body as code. That is a hole only if the script runs its stdin as a shell. Two headers in one segment share one owner after a lone `&`, but the detached-launch rule denies that line. `_RUN_LAUNCHERS` is one more runner list, beside RUNNER_WORDS in guard.py, SCRIPT_RUNNERS in guard_stream.py, ENV_NPM_RUNNERS in guard_env.py and ENV_CHILD_VALUE_FLAGS. Decision: env-file-stays-unread. Fix: none — a false alarm costs one rewritten command, and a missed launcher never opens a hole. Until: 2026-11-22.
- env-deno-run-stdin: `deno run --env-file=ENV -` reads a stdin script but allows, since `run` reads as the script word (hooks/guard_env.py _runner_inline_code). `bun run -` with an env file, and `cat s.js |& node --env-file=ENV` (a pipe with `|&`), also run inline code with the file loaded. Fix: none — Claude rarely feeds a stdin script to deno run -, bun run -, or pipes with |&. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-value-flag-list-closed: a node value flag outside ENV_RUNNER_VALUE_FLAGS, such as `--title x -e y`, reads its value as the script and allows (hooks/guard_env.py). Fix: none — the value-flag list is closed by design, and an unlisted value flag before -e is rare. Until: 2026-11-21
- env-find-sort-files0-from: `sort --files0-from=-` and `--compress-program` after a find listing read or run what the name list holds, and `ENV_FIND_FILTERS` lists `sort` whole (hooks/guard_env.py ENV_FIND_FILTERS). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-heredoc-last-find-text-ok: a find name test as the last substitution of an unquoted heredoc body gets `text_ok`, so the name test allows (hooks/guard_env.py env_refusal, the `subs_only` cut). Fix: none — only an attacker writes this form (guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-21
- env-split-string-wsl-only: only the WSL rule reads the env -S script; draft-pr, detached-launch, rm-var and impeccable-review rules do not (hooks/guard_core.py nested_script). Fix: none — a review finding with no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- estimate-rule-may-be-checkable: the measured time table (debt `measured-time-table`) may let a check compare a stated time with a measured range (row style-no-unmeasured-estimate) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-25
- expired-delete-net-error-stops: the expired-lock delete in GhLock.acquire runs once, so a network error or rate limit stops the run with NetStop (merge/merge.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- fb-inbox-append-race: concurrent ab appends to the feedback inbox on Windows lost 406 of 8000 lines in a stress test (hooks/local_time.py) Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-20
- guard-exit-7-unprobed: exit 7 under onFailure block was never probed; a timeout was measured to refuse the call (guard wrappers) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- guard-own-errors-fail-open: guard and rm_prompt_answer exit 0 on their own internal errors (guard scripts) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- ghlock-no-compare-and-swap: GitHub API has no compare-and-swap; an expiring lock between read and PATCH could overwrite a rival's lock (merge/merge.py GhLock) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- git-env-glued-block: a glued PowerShell block opener followed by a group hides a git-env set from the guard (hooks/guard_kill.py) Fix: none — owner decides (decision guard-guards-mistakes-only). Until: 2026-11-24
- git-env-lower-case-fold: lower-case git env names fold to the rule; no record names this as accepted (guard hooks) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- git-env-set-false-alarms: safe forms such as try{$env:GIT_DIR=$null}catch{} and Write-Host {$env:GIT_DIR=1} are refused (hooks/guard_kill.py)
- git-env-set-indirect-forms: indirect sets (function wrapper, iex, EncodedCommand, run-time names) pass the git-env guard (hooks/guard_kill.py) Fix: none — owner decides (decision git-env-set-refused). Until: 2026-11-19
- git-env-warn-session-start: the git env warning runs at session start only, so mid-session damage shows at next start (guard hooks) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- glued-subshell-unread: (rm -rf .) and x=$(tail -f y) pass because the closing parenthesis is glued to the last word (read hooks/guard_core.py GLUED_OPEN; hooks/guard_stream.py, hooks/guard_push_delete.py); `(git checkout -B x)` passes rule two-worktrees-one-branch the same way (read hooks/guard_branch_worktree.py). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-19
- guard-abtests-test-path: native/ABTests/ reads as a test folder, so a builder cannot edit product code there (hooks/guard.py)
- guard-audit-agent-stop-open: SubagentStop fails open on a missing guard module, on purpose, with one log line (hooks/guard.py) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- guard-bash-c-silent-write: a silent git write inside bash -c '...' is allowed; the check reads only the outer command (hooks/guard.py)
- guard-cd-walk-extra-reading: after an unclosed quote the second reading can hold a cd bash never runs, so a git write is judged in the wrong folder (guard_core _run_dir)

- guard-substitution-in-token: open items only. The command_indexes callers (guard_stream, guard_wsl, guard_push_delete, question_form) miss a glued substitution. An inner `cd` is judged against the session root (`echo $(cd /x && git reset --hard)`). Rm after cd reads `stripped` only, so `$(cd x && rm -rf y)` is not read. A `$( )` in a quoted interpreter heredoc body is judged as shell, a possible false alarm. Fix: step:substitution-in-token-callers

- merge-lock-expired-delete-race: GhLock (the Mac) deletes an expired lock with no compare-and-swap. So it can remove a live lock that another run just took, and then two runs hold the lock. The window is a few API calls. A cloud run releases the lock to an expired commit, never deletes it, so every Mac take after a cloud merge goes through this window. Fix: the Mac also uses GitLock, whose leased push is compare-and-swap (merge/merge.py GhLock.acquire). Decision: none

- watch-flat-names-win: config_watch puts its own folder before the guard.d set on the import path, so a flat shared_names.py left stale by a failed install wins over the set copy (hooks/config_watch.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- watch-reads-guard-each-call: the watch reads guard.py for its INSTALL_ID on every call, even on the fast exit; small cost (hooks/config_watch.py _add_guard_set_dir). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- watch-escaped-cap-key: a JSON-escaped cap key outside env is a lift to the guard but not to the fast path, so its expiry check is skipped; only an attacker writes it (hooks/config_watch.py lift_candidate). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- watch-bad-json-mixed-cap: in unparseable JSON the last text cap assignment wins, so opus then sonnet reads as Sonnet; such a file loads no settings (hooks/config_watch.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21

- drops-body-nested-fence: a 4-backtick fence that holds a 3-backtick fence closes on the 3 backticks, so lines after it read as body text. Decision: silent-undo-check-design (one check catches a merge or branch that silently undoes earlier work) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23

- verdict-prune-reused-temp-log: a caller that reuses a VERDICT_LOG named verdict-*.log inside the temp folder can lose it to another run's prune once it is 7 days idle (bin/verdict prune). Decision: none Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- delete-rule-quoted-text: the first DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE pattern skips quoted text only after echo, printf, Write-Host, Write-Output or git commit. A quoted split-flag delete after any other command, such as a gh `--body`, is still refused (hooks/guard_push_delete.py _quotes_are_text). Fix: none — a wrapper such as parallel, script -c, watch or runuser -c runs its quoted text, and no list of them is whole. Decision: rm-path-wrapper (an rm shim on PATH, with the guard holding the line on Windows)
- rm-guard-home-parents-pass: the guard refuses a recursive delete of the working folder and every folder above it. It does not refuse a folder above home when the working folder is outside it, so `rm -r C:/Users` passes from D:/proj. The Mac and Linux shim refuses it (hooks/guard_push_delete.py _parent_target, hooks/guard_rm_var.py _rm_target_bad). Checked on the two functions, not through a full guard run. - eol-scan-bom-env-u: the eol scan reads line 1 as ASCII. A UTF-8 BOM then hides a #! line. The scan also reads the name after `env -u` (as in `#!/usr/bin/env -S -u NAME bash`) as the interpreter. An extensionless shell script with either form is not scanned for CR, and a .sh name still is (install-bin-eol.test.ps1 Get-FirstLine, Get-Interpreter). - scratchpad-shared-per-session: the guard keys a scratchpad by session id, and an agent carries its parent's id, so every agent of one session shares one scratchpad. The run case and the overwrite case are set by decision `scratchpad-owner` (a guard refuses an agent that runs or overwrites another agent's scratchpad file; step `scratchpad-file-ownership` builds that guard). Only another session's scratchpad is refused (read hooks/guard.py role_write_hit, hooks/guard_shared_tree.py under_session_scratchpad). - job-caps-scan-limits: job_caps keeps a trailing "# comment" in a job name's id, and a 4-space-indented workflow gives no floor and no warning; other repos' workflows were not read (merge/merge.py job_caps). Decision: none (skill ci-hygiene step 5) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24

- cloud-mirror-sha-write-fails: if the `claude-settings.mirrorSha` write fails after a good checkout, the log says main moved but could not be recorded, and mirrorSha stays old. The next refresh that finds origin moved checks full history: it moves main on if origin holds it; otherwise it keeps main, with a "could not tell" WARN if the fetch fails, or a "local commits" WARN if origin lost it (install.sh mirror_refresh).
- cloud-tarball-curl-no-cap: after a cloud clone fails at its 40 s cap, the tarball fallback's `curl -fsSL` has no `--max-time`. The run can then pass the hook's 60 s cap. Files land whole, so the old install stays (install.sh tarball fallback). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- cloud-mirror-sha-write-fails: if the `claude-settings.mirrorSha` write fails after a good checkout, the log says "could not refresh" though main moved, and later resumes warn of local commits and keep main (install.sh cloud resume). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- checkout-at-raw-origin: `checkout_at` reads the raw origin URL, so an alias origin such as `gh:owner/repo` with insteadOf no longer matches in a piped local install, which then fetches the tarball (install.sh checkout_at). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- cloud-mirror-skip-not-cloud-only: the freshness skip reads only the cloudMirror marker, so a marked clone stays silent on staleness when the refresh did not run. Main's tarball copy had no check either (hooks/session_start.sh). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- cloud-swap-old-left: a run killed between the two `mv` calls of the clone swap leaves `claude-settings.old.<pid>`, which the stale sweep (`claude-settings.clone.*` only) never removes (install.sh cloud clone). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- open-box-no-wake-to-page: a session in an open question box with nothing running gets no wake, so its question reaches the page only once the owner answers. Decision: questions-go-to-owner Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- waiting-page-gutter-cancel-keyboard-untested: no test covers the keyboard-click pass (`detail === 0`) of the picture's scrollbar-press cancel; removing it leaves the suite green (skills/waiting-page/page.html optPic). Spec: docs/design/c204-sizes.md item 4 Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- waiting-page-gutter-cancel-script-click: a left scrollbar press with no click keeps the cancel armed. Then a scripted or assistive-tech click with `detail` > 0 and no pointerdown is swallowed; a mouse or touch cannot make one (skills/waiting-page/page.html optPic). Spec: docs/design/c204-sizes.md item 4 Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- step-grant-revoke-stale: a grant that triage removes on origin/roadmap still lifts the Opus spawn refusal in a lane that has not fetched since (hooks/override.py _take_step). Decision: agent-model-cap Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- step-grant-one-bad-entry: one malformed entry in a step's `grants` blocks every grant on that step, a fail-safe false ask (hooks/override.py _take_step). Decision: agent-model-cap Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- probe-design-screens-by-route-count: lint/compliance_probes.py probe_design grades "screens named" by route count, so a shoot.json with one route passes when an app has three views. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- glossary-prepared-cache-by-identity: check_glossary `_PREPARED` caches by table identity and length, so an in-place edit of a table that keeps its length reads stale names. Production builds the table once (lint/check_glossary.py prepared). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- words-deadline-late-by-one-scan: WORDS_DEADLINE is checked between files and inside name_problems, not inside one file's scan, so a stop can run late by one scan: 23 s on a 42 MB JSON (lint/compliance_probes.py probe_words). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- ios-probe-quoted-name-with-space: the iOS probe reads quote marks as nothing, so `-scheme "Tally UI"` reads as `Tally`; when `Tally` is a unit-only scheme, a UI run passes. The starter bars spaces in names, so only a legacy repo hits it (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- ios-probe-quoted-separator: a quoted `;`, `|` or `&` inside an xcodebuild argument splits the command, so `-destination "a;b" test` drops `test`. No xcodebuild flag takes one (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- ios-probe-quoted-substitution-open: a lone `$(` in quotes, as in `-derivedDataPath '$(' test`, opens a group and drops `test`. Fix: none — no xcodebuild argument holds a lone `$(`, and quoted text must stay read as commands for `bash -c '...'` (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs).
- session-home-unknown-dir-write: under a lost session-home record, `cd "$WT" && echo x > f` is refused while `cd "$WT" && git commit` passes; an unknown folder counts as the primary for shell writes only (hooks/guard.py session_home_hit). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- session-home-agent-named-lane: a worktree named `agent-<x>` by EnterWorktree is read as an agent tree, so the session gets no record and stays unguarded (hooks/guard.py session_home_agent_tree). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- session-home-worktrees-folder: a git verb run from `<primary>/.claude/worktrees` itself, outside any lane, acts on the primary and passes (hooks/guard.py session_home_hit `in_primary`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- merge-role-skips-raw-merge: raw `gh pr merge` (permission-allowed) and the GitHub MCP merge tool skip the merge role check (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- card-scroll-margin-no-safe-area: .card, .tier and .bundle scroll-margin-top is 64px with no top safe-area inset, while .hdr sits at that inset (skills/waiting-page/page.html) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ste-changed-no-checkout-msg: when the installed hooks lack guard_pointer.py, ste-changed swallows the ImportError and says no checkout was found, blaming the pointer, not the missing module (bin/ste-changed find_root). Decision: action-pins-follow-sessions
- ste-changed-root-order-untested: the install cases in actions/ste-lint/test_action.py cannot tell own-checkout-first from pointer-first, since both checkouts hold the same history; they also add a third test-temp sweep proof. Decision: action-pins-follow-sessions Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- shoot-seed-fail-open: when the localStorage write throws, the shot runs unseeded and can pass on the wrong screen; Chromium's top frame on http(s) does not throw (bin/shoot seedStorage). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- shoot-own-context-newpage: a seeded or stepped route's own context stays open until browser close when newPage throws; the run aborts with exit 2, so nothing leaks (bin/shoot main). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- shoot-loading-swap-wall-clock: the no-steps fixture swaps content 10 s after load (`connecting(10000)`), still on the wall clock. A runner stalled over 10 s turns it red with no defect, and a shot delayed 2-10 s passes (lint/test_shoot.mjs). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command.
- install-move-mid-operation: install.sh moves a clean, detached checkout even with a bisect, rebase or cherry-pick stopped on it; skip when rebase-merge, rebase-apply, BISECT_LOG, CHERRY_PICK_HEAD, REVERT_HEAD or MERGE_HEAD is present (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers.
- install-move-status-exit: the checkout move reads `git status --porcelain` with its exit code ignored, so a failing status reads as clean (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- install-move-ignored-file: the checkout move overwrites an ignored local file when main tracks that path (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- install-move-test-gaps: caseM1-M4 have no shallow detached checkout, no failed-fetch case and no local-mode case (hooks/test_install_src.sh). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- win-rest-runner-hang: batch 23 #840 checks-windows-rest: the Windows runner stopped mid-job twice (no log, step timeout never fired); same head passed on re-run; cause outside repo code, unmeasured. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-26
- layout-check-deleted-cwd: lint/check_layout.py with no --root and a deleted cwd (a removed worktree) prints a Traceback, exit 1, not its one refusal line; it never passes. Decision: one-layout-every-repo. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- layout-test-worktree-prune: lint/test_layout.py Identity runs `git worktree prune` on the real repo's git folder, which also drops stale worktree records of other sessions; unmeasured race. Decision: one-layout-every-repo. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- feedback-guard-agent-burst: hooks/feedback_guard.py classes an agent hand-back as an owner burst and blocks Stop until it gets an item line (two blocks in lane wp-fit-w-one-home). Fix: none — rare and harmless: main already skips hand-back prompts (hooks/local_time.py:205), and the incident prompt is not in the repo. Decision: none.
- wp-field-warning-margin: the unknown-field disclosure's `<p>` keeps browser margins; `.status .cmd-more p { margin: 0 }` would tighten it. Decision: item-field-rendered. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- wp-field-list-template-only: the item-field-rendered guard reads the template page.html, not the live page; an empty or dropped block silently stops it (CI's real-page case goes red). Decision: item-field-rendered. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- chat-press-scan-after-lose: the chat press scan strips from `Gain:` to the end of the description, so a press written after `Lose:` passes with no link (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- mockup-replacestate-sandbox: nine docs/design mockups (c135-multi, c190-option-links, c190-task-command, c193-pics, c193-rail, c193-receipt, c193-sent, one-page, waiting-page) call history.replaceState with no catch. It throws in the card's sandboxed srcdoc frame, so a design with a `state` draws nothing there. Fixed in c204-scroll only (lane c204-pics). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- role-handoff-watch-none-wording: role-handoff.md should say `Watch: none` only when the role owns no page; PR #858 (triage watches its page) allows it whenever `Open items: none` (skills/project-ops/templates/role-handoff.md). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- op-model-hand-off-file-pinned: operating-model.md keeps the spelling "hand-off file" because skills/project-ops/tests/test_scaffold.py pins it; renaming it to "handoff" needs those pins moved in the same change. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- report-guard-model-name-next: lint/report_guard.py flags an agent named only under Next as a Done item with no model, even as "(builder sonnet)". It fired 4 times in lane handoff-no-copied-lists, all false alarms. Decision: none.
- op-model-wait-with-home: operating-model.md rotation step 3 says the file names each wait it handed over. A wait with a home, such as CI on a PR, can read as a copy. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- none-direction-note-only-answer: `is_reask` still reads a task answer that is a note with no `done` (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md, `answers/<id>`) written with new `options` as a re-ask, so the page judge gets the none rule there. Kept: the fix covers a `done` answer only (hooks/call_judge.py). Decision: question-reviewer-agent-hook
- check-handoff-id-letter-prefix: skills/project-ops/scripts/check_handoff.py passes `<URL> A12, B14` and `<URL> #A12`, since its ID pattern needs no word character before the digits. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- check-handoff-bracket-after-url: skills/project-ops/scripts/check_handoff.py checks brackets only in the first URL, so `<URL> <placeholder>` passes where main refused it. A real home is still named. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- wp-ruling-draft-note: skills/waiting-page/page.html setDraft merges, so an unsent note typed while an item was open joins its overturn once it is a ruling; the ruling card shows no note box. Found by reading, not run. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- wp-rulings-never-leave: a closed arbiter ruling stays on the waiting page for good, so the "Arbiter rulings" section grows on long runs. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-23
- designer-steps-test-one-sentence: hooks/test_blind_brief.py DesignerSteps wants the critique order as one sentence with `run`, `impeccable` and `critique`. A split, "invoke" for "run", or the order joined to the blind-lane Setup skip goes red, with a clear message. Decision: design-skills. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- frontend-design-cloud-missing: the `frontend-design` plugin is on in settings.json but not installed in cloud containers, so the Skill tool does not know it. Designers and builders read its SKILL.md file from the marketplace folder (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: design-skills. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- designer-worktree-auto-removed: a designer that writes only to the scratchpad leaves its worktree unchanged, so the harness removes it at the end of the run. Guard `worktree-home` then refuses a resume, and each fix round needs a fresh designer (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- blind-lane-on-redraw: the blind-brief hook refuses a directed designer with no `Blind lanes:` line, also on a redraw of a concept the owner picked. The lane must add a blind lane or quote the owner (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-19
- scratchpad-shared-across-lanes: designer lanes of one session share its scratchpad, so a blind lane saw the directed lane's contrast script with its colours. It did not use them (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- impeccable-launcher-refused: guard `impeccable-review-only` refuses the impeccable launcher and detector. So the designer's critique runs in one context with no detector (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: design-skills. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-21
- wp-field-list-arbiter-unpinned: no test pins `arbiter` or `ruled` in the itemFields block (hooks/test_guard.py IF_REQUIRED, page.test.mjs field check), and the ruling card never warns on an unknown field. Decision: item-field-rendered. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- reviewer-model-claim: two lane reviewers reported themselves as haiku, though the spawn calls set model sonnet (Oct 30; lane coord-other-repo-compliance was round 1, so the frontmatter model may have won; lane wp-fit-w-one-home round unknown); whether the spawn's model held is unverified. - page-queued-shape-assumed: the guard anchors an owner message queued mid-turn on its `queued_command` record. - page-block-no-closed-hint: the block reason for a `[page]` item with no send does not name `[closed]` as the way out when no send is possible, such as a dead writer (lane page-answer-send). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox.
- still-after-tap-cannot-catch-scroll: the 8 stillAfterTap cases fire DOM clicks, and no page code scrolls on a tap, so they catch a page-code scroll only and never a native focus scroll (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25

- near-cards-comments-stale: the NEAR_CARDS cases name a 64px option scroll margin and a header gap guard that the page lacks, so their 10px cases pass trivially (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- stale-step-partial-walk: the roadmap stale-step row cannot tell a one-page `list_sessions` copy from a full walk (`has_more` is true after 30 rows), so lanes on later pages read stale; SKILL.md says "every page". - merge-role-proof-crash-fails-open: a crash in judge() fails open (hooks/guard.py main), so with the exact `merge --role` allow rules a crash in role_proof_hit leaves no other judge on the call (hooks/guard_merge.py). It becomes a debt after a real incident. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- merge-role-proof-trusts-tag: guard rule `merge-role-proof` trusts the session's cs-build tag, so a tag set by mistake, with set_session_tags or copied at spawn, gives the role (hooks/guard_merge.py role_proof_hit). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- same-name-check-order-unmeasured: a dispatch run on a PR head posts a skipped `all-jobs-passed` beside an earlier red one. merge.py reads red; how GitHub's own protection orders two same-name check runs is unmeasured (lane all-jobs-passed-rollup). Main had this gap. Decision: hold-unsafe-after-round-3. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-26
- role-model-unranked-branch-no-mutant: the unranked-model branch of guard rule agent-role-model has no mutant in hooks/mutate_guard.py; the suite still catches its removal (3 red cases). Decision: agent-model-cap. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- map-uses-bare-name-any-import: a bare name counts as a use when the cited file is imported for side effects only, so `import {notes} from "./other"; import "./notes"; notes` passes `uses ...:notes`, in JS and Python (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- map-uses-py-deep-chain: Python counts `retry.cfg.X` as a use of `X` from retry, but JS rejects `m.other.notes` (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/py-facts.py). Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- map-uses-renamed-import: a renamed import (`from retry import X as Z`, `{ notes as n }`) reads "never uses" (2 of 681 uses in this repo). Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- map-uses-reexport-python: a Python re-export (hooks/guard_core.py re-exports 22 git_reads names) reads "never uses", while JS `export {x} from` reads as a use. Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- map-blankshell-limits-unstated: blankShell reads `let y=1<<2` as a heredoc and never ends a CRLF heredoc (`EOF\r`), so the rest of the file reads gone. An unclosed `((` stops heredoc reading, and a quote inside `$()` in a double-quoted string ends that string. None of these is in the code or the decision's Limits section (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/source-text.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- map-js-uses-limits-unstated: `require(x).sym`, `{ default as lib }`, `ns["x"]` and a shadowed alias are not read, and the code and the decision do not say so (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- map-blankshell-paths-untested: no test case covers `<<-`, a quoted heredoc word, two heredocs on one line, `$'\''`, or `#` after `;&|()<>`, all proved by probe only. The test names at skills/plain-english-map/tests/fact-lines.test.mjs R1 and R2 say "today", which goes stale. Decision: map-facts-are-checked. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- map-pin-two-senses: "pins the install link" and the quoted "pinned copies" use "pin" in two senses; "links the skill, never copies it" reads plainer (docs/decisions/map-skill-lives-here.md, three debt glosses). Decision: map-skill-lives-here. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- map-installer-copies-unstated: cloud and refused-symlink installs still make a copy, and skills/plain-english-map/SKILL.md treats that copy as supported; the record does not say such copies are installer-made and belong to debt skill-copy-no-typescript. Decision: map-skill-lives-here. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- map-older-copy-means-version: skills/plain-english-map/SKILL.md says "an older copy of the skill" where it means an older version. Decision: map-skill-lives-here. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- question-departs-edges: `lint/report_guard.py` LABEL_RE skips a bold "**Departs from:**" line, so one outside Input Needed can give a Done item its PR ref or sit before Done. A change side of only "<what changes>." with a period passes. A `str_replace` with no label in old or new is not judged. A label in mid-line is refused. Decision: port-keeps-behaviour. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- trust-card-in-build-same-message-order: a `get_session` and a `set_session_tags` in one message can be read in the wrong order, so the guard misjudges the tags. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- trust-card-in-build-denial-no-cause: a fail-closed denial logs no cause in guard.log, so the reason is lost. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- set-waiting-page-usage-exit: `layout.py set-waiting-page` with the wrong number of arguments exits 1, the code that also means "already set". Decision: session-tiers.
- set-waiting-page-new-root: a mistyped root makes `<root>/.claude/layout-extras.json` in a new folder and exits 0. Decision: session-tiers.
- record-branch-null-stops: `record-branch: null` stops the roadmap build, while `waiting-page: null` counts as unset. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- reap-log-done-by-margin: janitor/agent_end_reap.py LOG_DONE_BY 58 leaves 2 s, but the hook's 60 s timer starts before `started` (shell, interpreter start, stdin read); 55 was proposed. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- log-lock-retries-every-oserror: _log_lock retries on every OSError, so a non-contention error (ENOLCK, EOPNOTSUPP) waits to the deadline; a contention-only retry was proposed but its Windows errno pair (13, 36) for msvcrt.locking is unverified. Decision: c95. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-22
- command-file-backticked: a `command` with both shells excuses "Run the command in `plans/c152.md`", because the file name sits in backticks (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- command-run-verb-script-path: a run verb earlier in the sentence makes a script path such as `./hooks/guard.py` read as a shell line, as in "Run the tests and open `./hooks/guard.py`" (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- test-git-ps1-twin: the PowerShell suites (install.test.ps1 and the install-* .ps1 tests) commit in fixtures with no lint/_test_git twin. They run on Windows CI, not on the cloud signer. Step: test-runs-skip-signing. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-25
- test-git-bad-count-reset: lint/_test_git.py/.sh/.mjs reset a non-numeric GIT_CONFIG_COUNT to 0 and drop the preset keys silently. Step: test-runs-skip-signing. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- page-title-scan-edges: `splice.title_span` takes the last markup `<title>` outside an svg, so a later markup title wins, and a self-closing or unclosed `<svg` before it gives no title (carry keeps the template's; stale reads behind). page.html has one markup title, so no real page reaches either (lane page-name-queue). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- c204-clamp-untested: in `fitFrame` the scroll width clamp `pw = min(ceil(bw × sc), room)` has no test that goes red without it. Float error makes ceil pass the room by 1px only at rooms such as 202, 203, 214 and 215, which no tested width reaches (lane c204-scroll-width, review round 2). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- standing-grant-local-unenforced: merge/merge.py validates `merge.standingGrant` on every run but enforces it only in a cloud session. A local session's `merge <pr> --confirm` merges with the switch false. There the tool cannot tell the owner from a session. The owner must stay able to merge the PR that turns the switch back on. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-21
- merge-put-bypass: no guard refuses a direct `gh api -X PUT .../pulls/<n>/merge`, and the cloud proxy passes it. That skips the merge tool's role check and its `merge.standingGrant` switch. A debt after an incident. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- standing-grant-read-once: merge/merge.py reads `merge.standingGrant` once, before the lock. A switch set to false during that run's lock wait or checks wait does not stop the run. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- cloud-setup-timeout-orphan: when `cloud-setup.sh` hits its 240 s cap, the kill may not reach install.sh's inner `timeout 40 git`. That git can run up to 40 s on, and install.sh's lock may stay, so the next install within 10 minutes fails. Found by code reading, never run (lane setup-script-install, review round 1). Decision: agent-model-cap. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- cloud-setup-origin-copy: `origin_is_repo` in cloud-setup.sh is a looser copy of install.sh's `origin_matches_repo`, because install.sh runs on load and cannot be sourced. It differs only on contrived URLs, and no test pins the two together (lane setup-script-install). Decision: agent-model-cap. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- cloud-setup-zombie-step: before its group kill, cloud-setup.sh first sends TERM to each child of the install, then waits 1 s. The step is there for test c2 only, because this sandbox's pid 1 never clears dead children, so a dead child still looks alive. The better fix is for c2 to count a dead child (state Z) as gone, and then drop the step (lane setup-script-install, review round 2). Decision: agent-model-cap. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- holder-slash-unpinned: nothing pins "no / in HOLDER" (janitor/test_session_end_sweep.py); a later "/" breaks Windows only, under MSYS path rewriting, while Linux stays green. Decision: c95. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-19
- sweep-no-pipes-kwargs-only: SweepGetsNoPipes pins the DEVNULL kwargs; no case has a grandchild holding a pipe, so a hang by another route passes unseen. A Windows-only grandchild case would make it red-capable. Decision: c95. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-20
- heartbeat-starved-reads-stopped: a live holder starved 1 s or more reads as stopped, a false green only when the kill also failed. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- kill-holder-rekills-dead-pids: kill_holder and the atexit stop_holders kill each pid again, dead or not; a recycled pid inside that seconds-wide window could be killed. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- scratchpad-reap-reads-ps: ScratchpadReap reads liveness with ps, not the heartbeat; the reap is POSIX-only and needs ps to find orphans. Decision: c95. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-23
- reap-start-row-budget-unbound: janitor/agent_end_reap.py's start row give_up_at (HOOK_BUDGET, 45 s) never binds, because LOG_WAIT_SECONDS (30 s) ends the wait first; the hook-bound case tests a clamp production never reaches. The 60 s bound holds through the done row. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- hook-bound-case-1s-slack: janitor/test_sweep.py's hook-bound case allows 4.0 s on an expected 3.0 s; a stall over 1 s in main() on a loaded runner reads red. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- lock1sh-first-holder-unproven: hooks/test_install_src.sh lock1sh does not prove install A took the lock first; under load B can go first and the case passes uncontested. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- win-lk-lock-regression-uncovered: on Windows the holder holds 5 s, under LK_LOCK's own 10 s give-up, so only the POSIX stand-in can catch an LK_LOCK regression. Decision: c95. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-19
- sendmessage-state-skips-at-cap: hooks/test_sendmessage_state.py still skips (NOT-RUN) when a slow runner reaches its harness cap; the step's goal says never skipped. Decision: c95. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- ci-job-no-pointer-unknown: a copied install with no pointer checkout reads a remote code-check step unknown, not FAIL; exit 2 still blocks a pass (bin/ci-job code_check). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- ci-job-interrupt-leaves-verdict: Ctrl-C during a step leaves bin/verdict and the step's shell running; the step's children outlived it on main too (bin/ci-job heartbeat relay). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- ci-job-relay-locale-decode: the heartbeat relay reads verdict's UTF-8 output in the locale's text mode, so a non-cp1252 byte on Windows can raise mid-step (bin/ci-job). Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-23
- ci-job-local-action-other-path: LOCAL_ACTION matches only `$/actions/` and `./actions/`, so a local action at another path reads as remote and borrows a copy; same on main (bin/ci-job). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- ci-job-cwd-tree: `bin/ci-job` runs each step in the session's working tree, not in a clean copy of the head (bin/ci-job main). Fix: none — a review finding with no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- test-ci-job-pipx-probe-windows: the pipx probe's 60 s timeout on Windows can wait on a pipx child that holds the pipe (lint/test_ci_job.py). Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-25
- test-ci-job-archive-head: the in-repo code-check case runs `git archive HEAD`, so a local run tests only committed bin/ci-job edits (lint/test_ci_job.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- confirm-cites-deadline: the ConfirmWaitCites deadline case has no assertNotIn for GREEN, so a regression that prints GREEN there passes (open PR #1485, merge/test_merge.py ConfirmWaitCites). Fix: none — a test gap in open PR code; no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- confirm-cites-base-ignore: ConfirmWaitCites has no base or ignoreChecks case (open PR #1485, merge/test_merge.py ConfirmWaitCites). Fix: none — a test gap in open PR code; no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- confirm-cites-whole-const: ConfirmWaitCites asserts whole constants, not the cite text they carry (open PR #1485, merge/test_merge.py ConfirmWaitCites). Fix: none — a test gap in open PR code; no incident. Until: 2026-11-22
- map-path-hard-coded: skills/plain-english-map and its map-check write and read "docs/map" as a literal, not the layout kind `map` through lint/layout.py. Decision: one-layout-every-repo. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-19
- map-check-double-install: when the mods flag is on, the checks job runs `npm ci` twice on one lockfile, in .github/scripts/map-check.sh and in .github/scripts/mod-tests.sh; `npm ci` wipes node_modules, so the second never skips. - merge-progress-stdout-only: merge/merge.py prints its progress line to stdout only, so `merge ... | grep` hides it from the stall watch; no incident (review round 1 of PR #932). Decision: stall-watch. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21

- install-cloud-budget-untested: no test drives install.sh's 50 s cloud_git budget or the OWN_CHECKOUT backup note, so deleting either stays green (install.sh). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26

- lane-fetch-before-mirror: a clean lane branch ahead of main pays one origin fetch, up to the 40 s cap, before the mirror clone. One case (hooks/guard_frozen.py). Decision: frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- test-guard-new-constant-comment: the comment above EXPLORE_MARKER in hooks/test_guard.py (near line 20008) says the guard constant "is new", which goes stale once that constant merges. Test files need a builder. Decision: agent-model-cap
- page-judge-title-only: an items update that carries neither options nor context, such as a title-only write, is not model-judged, so a bare id in a title passes (same source). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- page-judge-partial-update: an items update with `context` and no `kind` is judged as a decision even when the stored item is a task. A context-only update may cite ids glossed only in the unseen item, so it may be flagged under the no-unexplained-ids item. The owner override lifts it (review round 3 of lane page-question-judge) (hooks/question_form.py judged_item_writes, hooks/call_judge.py PAGE_PROMPT). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- question-form-approval-noun: `the owner's approval note` is read as an owner act and refused, since only a state verb after `approval` clears it (hooks/question_form.py OWNER_ACT). Fix: none — a false alarm only refuses. Until: 2026-11-22
- question-form-merge-queue: `the owner's merge-queue` is read as an owner act and refused, since a hyphen after `merge` ends the word (hooks/question_form.py OWNER_ACT). Fix: none — a false alarm only refuses. Until: 2026-11-22
- page-judge-dead-fragment-fields: _judged_fragment still accepts `options` and `options.N` subfields, which the ArtifactData str_replace schema cannot send (one top-level string key); dead code, harmless (review round 1 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- page-judge-mixed-batch: a decision item in a batch with a task item should keep its Goal, Problem and pick checks under PAGE_PROMPT's task exemption. It is unmeasured, because the real-model probe ran single items only (review round 3 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- page-judge-task-ids: task steps are now judged on unexplained ids, so a step that names a branch, sha or command may get a false alarm. It is unmeasured, and the owner override lifts it (review round 3 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- page-design-band-comment: test comments call the 390x56 band "near 1" in scale, but it measures 0.75 (skills/waiting-page/tests/test_check_designs.mjs). Decision: page-item-design. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- page-design-stamp-path: the published stamp holds a path, not a copy, so an edit to that file after the publish changes what the next item write is checked against (hooks/guard.py, skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py). Decision: page-item-design. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- page-design-closed-update: an update with no status to a closed item is still checked, though the page never draws closed items (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: page-item-design. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- ps1-copy-skill-prune-no-backup: in copy mode, install.ps1 prunes files inside a copied skill in place, while install.sh backs the copy up first (install.ps1 Install-Dir). Decision: copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup
- ps1-copy-skill-prune-no-backup: in copy mode, install.ps1 prunes files inside a copied skill in place, while install.sh backs the copy up first. The copy-mode refresh and the copy-to-link swap also delete an owner's file added inside a shipped skill folder (install.ps1 Install-Dir). Decision: copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup
- install-sh-unchecked-skill-mv: install.sh moves an old skill backup with an unchecked `mv` (install.sh move_old_skill_baks). Decision: install-manifest-prunes. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- install-tarball-no-prune: an install from a source with no .git counts every file in the extracted tree as shipped, so a file main removed stays in place (install.sh tarball fallback). Decision: install-manifest-prunes. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- install-first-run-rm-no-retry: on the first run with no manifest, a failed delete of a listed link into the clone is not held back. The manifest is then written, and the link is never retried (install.sh retire_listed). Decision: install-manifest-prunes. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- install-ps1-nonascii-names: the install.ps1 post-merge hook reads git without -z and the installer decodes -z output with the console encoding, so a non-ASCII shipped name could be skipped. No such name ships now. Decision: install-manifest-prunes. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-20
- install-manifest-test-gaps: no test runs install.ps1's manifest code outside CI, none feeds git a failure mid-install, and none uses odd file names. Decision: install-manifest-prunes. Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-21
- option-letter-keys-label-word-tag: a label that opens with a word tag other than Option, such as "Approach B:" or "Plan B:", passes LABEL_TAG. It is kept out because "Plan B" is plain English (hooks/question_form.py LABEL_TAG; decision option-keys-are-letters). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- report-guard-head-omits-pr: BLOCK_REASON says "commit, push, or merge" and leaves out an opened PR, a revert, an update-branch and a file delete, which the guard also checks (lint/report_guard.py). Found in PR #966 round 3.
- report-guard-gh-value-flags: the no-write flag scan skips no value flags, so a create whose title is `-w` is missed and `-fw` reads as landing; reuse PR_CREATE_VALUE_FLAGS (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- report-guard-glued-repo-flag: a glued `-Ro/r` reads repo empty in merge_call_of, so the root form is judged against the cwd repo; no test pins it (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- pw-os-release-source-fail: pin.sh sources os-release under `set -eu` with stderr hidden, so a file with shell logic or an unset reference ends the step with no message. Real os-release files hold none (actions/playwright-setup/pin.sh). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- pw-os-release-env-name: pin.sh's test hook `OS_RELEASE` is a generic env name. A job that sets it for another reason gets `os=Linux` and a warning (actions/playwright-setup/pin.sh). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- pw-os-test-gaps: test_action.py has no case for an os-release with no ID, and no check that a normal Ubuntu run prints no warning (actions/playwright-setup/test_action.py). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- pw-26-04-cause-unread: the decision cites `libicudata.so.74` on 26.04 from the joyous lane's report, not a log this lane read (docs/decisions/one-browser-setup-no-apt.md). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- page-stale-empty-live: a live page that is itself untouched (placeholder title, empty blocks) makes `--write` build a page `page-stale` refuses, because the guard cannot read the live page. The exit is the title: the refusal tells the session to set the page `<title>` to "<repo> Cockpit" (owner ruling, 2026-11-02 11:12 CST) and republish (hooks/guard.py PAGE_EMPTY_REASON). Decision: page-stale-publish
- roadmap-quoted-push-passes: a push whose words are all quoted (`git push 'origin' 'roadmap'`) passes roadmap-one-writer, because PUSH_QUOTED joins them (hooks/guard_push_delete.py). Main's `force-push` rule has the same gap. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- own-session-cse-id-form: the own-id match in `_own_session_read` takes the `session_<x>` form only, so a `set_session_tags` call naming the `cse_<x>` form leaves the proof current (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- guard-comments-stale-wording: the hooks/guard.py comments at the rule catalogue (~line 85, trust-card) and at clause 6a2 (cost) still give the old wording. Fix them in the next lane that edits hooks/guard.py. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- origin-odd-forms-bogus-link: odd origins give a bogus slug and link: `../sibling`, Azure `_git/repo`, `github.com/o/n/pulls`. No test_build case pins a link for such an origin; its only link case uses a github.com origin (skills/roadmap/test_build.py). Decision: agent-model-cap. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- roll-out-lane-starting-tag: a roll-out lane carries the starting repo's tag beside its target's. The starting roadmap counts it live if its cs-step id matches one of its own steps, and step ids repeat across repos. Unverified. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- lane-wrong-cs-step-unjudged: a lane tagged with the wrong cs-step is not caught by lane-repo-tag, and a set_session_tags call after the start is not judged either. It may be a cause of the 2026-11-01 18:08 CST false flags (incident in agent-model-cap); unmeasured. Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-26
- page-parked-off-look-untested: no test covers the parked lines' off look (struck label, --faint edge, forced-colors GrayText) or the "from parked <ref>" bar text, so a CSS edit could make an off act look live (skills/waiting-page/page.html, skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs). Step: page-parked-actions. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- page-parked-badge-race: keepRouted shows the "Unparked" or "Dropped" badge only when the meta/page snapshot arrives before the answers delete; otherwise the line leaves at once. Harmless (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-parked-actions. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- page-why-noid-copy-wording: WHY_NOID copy could read "The writer has not set this line up. Reply in the thread." (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-parked-actions. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- recovered-sidechain-boundary: the Recovered guard's boundary scan does not skip `isSidechain` records, so an agent's compaction, if inlined in the main transcript, would demand the line once (lint/report_guard.py recovered_problem). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- recovered-rewrite-crash: in the rewrite path, a crash in recovered_problem reaches the outer except and drops the Unstarted and model reasons too (lint/report_guard.py rewrite_problems). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- recovered-missing-report-tail: the missing-report block does not name the Recovered line, so a compacted session gets a second block on the rewrite (read lint/report_guard.py BLOCK_REASON). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- recovered-prose-match: a `Recovered:` line in a mid-turn prose reply, or a report skipped after another Stop hook's block, satisfies or delays the demand (lint/report_guard.py CARRY_LINES). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- carry-start-separators: `CARRY_START` does not count `Handoff gave:` after ",", "and", a dash or a pipe. The demand stays pending (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- carry-none-forms: `(none)`, `[none]`, "no losses" and "n/a" are judged as Recovered items that need a source (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- carry-bare-wrap: under a bare `Recovered:`, a wrapped line or sub-bullet becomes an extra item with no source (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- carry-label-lookahead: the Recovered and Handoff gave exclusion in `LABEL_RE` has no word boundary, so `**Recovered work:**` is not read as a label. It passes report shape, and a bold carry line under Done joins the last Done item (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- carry-pre-deploy: a session compacted before the guard lands gets one demand at its first report (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- page-ruling-recite: the roadmap upkeep row "page rulings not on their step" lists a closed item again when a rewrite of its step note drops the `item <n>` cite (skills/roadmap/build.py, unfiled). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- page-ruling-free-home: that row matches only a bare step id or `step <id>` as an item's home, so a free-text home such as "c204 (zoomed picture)" is a silent miss (skills/roadmap/build.py, unfiled). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- page-ruling-doc-gaps: build.py's module docstring omits two unknown cases that SKILL.md lists (not a JSON object; no threads or pulls list), and no test covers them (skills/roadmap/build.py, test_build.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- dated-claim-year-digits: the dated-claims upkeep row reads any four digits after the day as a year. So "Checked Nov 1 1830 CDT" lists a fresh claim (skills/roadmap/build.py, CLAIM). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- dated-claim-dot-stops: that row stops at any ".", so "Checked hooks/guard.py on main Oct 20", "Oct. 28" and "Sept 28" are misses (skills/roadmap/build.py, CLAIM). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- dated-claim-edges: that row counts days in UTC, not Central. An explicit future year masks older claims. "_Checked_" is a miss. A future plan date after the word rolls to last year and lists (skills/roadmap/build.py, dated_claims). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- archive-no-record-any-repo: rule lane-closes-after-records denies any archive_session with no get_session record of the target, lane or not, in every repo; the steer costs one turn (hooks/guard_merge.py archive_hit). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- archive-tag-literals: archive_hit repeats the `cs-step:` and `cs-repo:` literals from skills/roadmap/build.py; a rename there fails closed with "no step or repo tag" (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- archive-rebased-lane-commit: rule lane-closes-after-records still refuses an archive when the lane's draft head was rebased before its merge, because the new hash is not the head. A repo that merges by squash or rebase never lands a lane head, so the release never fires there. A session on the owner's computer closes the draft (hooks/guard_merge.py archive_hit). Decision: session-tiers. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- repeat-inbox-source-shared-try: own and other-inbox blocks share one try, so an `os.listdir` error drops both sources (hooks/feedback_guard.py other_inbox_candidates). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- repeat-mark-lone-cr: mark_items reads bytes and parse reads text, so a lone CR in an owner message shifts item lines and the mark is reported "not marked" (hooks/feedback_guard.py mark_items). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- repeat-inbox-ref-same-minute: two blocks in one minute share an inbox ref, so the last label wins (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- repeat-mark-append-race: the inbox rewrite on a hit can lose a capture append that lands in the same instant; there is no lock (hooks/feedback_guard.py mark_items). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- marker-flags-unmutated: the `(?im)` flags on the BRIEF_MARKERS rows in hooks/shared_names.py have no mutant, because mutate_guard TARGETS has no shared_names entry.
