- lifted-row-refusal-skips-load-brake: in hooks/guard.py, a lifted above-row refusal (step 9b3, the role model row) exits refuse() at once, so the load brake (9c) is skipped. Only under an owner override. The 9c comment names this hazard for the Opus refusal only (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- role-key-not-sole-normaliser: role_key's docstring in hooks/guard.py calls it the one normaliser in this file, but role_has_model_pin folds role case on its own (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- model-cap-mutants-missing: hooks/mutate_guard.py has no mutant for the never_lifted no-model split, the Explore Audit: Opus grant, Explore leaving OPUS_STANDING_ROLES, or the literal-brief remedy lines (`BRIEF_LITERAL_REMEDY`, hooks/guard.py ~2206 role row and ~3416 Opus refusal). The tests in hooks/test_guard.py cover them. The mutant label at line ~862 says "denial" (glossary term: refuse) (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- explore-no-model-env-default: an Explore spawn with no model is not judged by `role_model_check` in hooks/guard.py (~line 2162) when `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` is set. Per the Claude Code docs ("Choose a model", checked on 2.1.316, 2026-11-02), the agent file's `model` frontmatter beats `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, and `agents/explore.md` pins haiku, so the spawn runs haiku. Only `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` lifts the pin; the settings-write check asks before that is set. No guard change. Owner waiting-page item 137, pick a (Nov 1 18:38 CST), covers the case. Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- workflow-unread-brief-false-alarms: hooks/guard.py refuses a Workflow `agent()` call whose brief is a variable, even when the variable holds the grant: an Explore with `Audit:`, a reviewer with `Round 2 of 3` or `Round 3 of 3`, a builder with `Round 3 of 3`. The override or a literal brief lifts it (coordinator ruling, Nov 1 22:54 CST). A real incident can promote reading the leading literal of a concatenation. The literal-brief remedy also shows on refusals no marker can pass: a test-author or record-writer row refusal, and a general-purpose or reviewer Opus refusal. Each costs one wasted retry. Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-23
- page-stale-untested-paths: no test pins `artifact_id` returning "" for a bare artifact url, or the `noted` line when the stamp write fails (hooks/guard.py page_stale_hit). Decision: page-stale-publish. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- page-stale-url-forms: the `/artifact/{id}` and `/code/artifact/{uuid}` links may carry different ids for one page; then the by-id compare matches neither across forms. Unmeasured. Decision: page-stale-publish. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- other-checkout-mkdir-touch: `touch` and `mkdir` are not in `MUTATING_COMMAND`, so a direct `mkdir <other checkout>/x` with no `cd` passes the write scan, and so does `mkdir -p <other>/d && cd <other>/d` (the `cd` target is made in the same command, so it does not exist when the guard reads it); the list is shared with frozen-path (hooks/guard.py `SHELL_WRITE_HINT`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- builders-reset-soft-refused: `reset --soft` to a cut point or to HEAD~N, then a commit, is refused; the squash and `cherry-pick -n` gap covers two of these forms (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- builders-own-test-commit-forgotten: an own test commit is forgotten after any rebase (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- builders-reflog-no-author: the reflog cannot name an author, so a shared worktree or the main checkout refuses another role's test commit, and commit and push skip stop's main-checkout check (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- builders-amend-ff-pull-refused: amending a fast-forward-pulled commit is refused (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- builders-untracked-test-folder-refused: an untracked file under a test folder is refused; other repos are unmeasured (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- builders-cost-line-untested: no test proves the Cost paragraph (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- builders-own-test-commit-forgotten: an own test commit is forgotten after any rebase (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests) Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- builders-cherry-pick-unlogged: ENTRY_RESOLVED does not match the subject `commit (cherry-pick):`, because `\b` after `)` never matches before `:`. So a cherry-pick the builder resolved by hand leaves no log line, though the decision says such picks are logged only. Fix idea: `(?::|\b)` (hooks/guard.py ENTRY_RESOLVED). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-rebase-start-unread: ENTRY_REBASE does not read a `pull -r ... (start)` subject as a rebase start, so no KEPT log line is written for that rebase. - builders-amend-replayed-test: amending a product edit into a replayed test-author commit is refused, because the amended commit's diff holds the test path (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- lease-fails-open: simulator-boot-lease allows an MCP `launch` when `simctl list` cannot be read, as the cap and reuse rules do on main. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- lease-name-two-runtimes: an MCP `launch` by a name that is booted on one runtime and stopped on another (or a name differing only in case) is refused, though the tool may pick the booted one. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-20
- lease-action-case: the guard judges only action `launch`; whether the simulator MCP takes `Launch` or a missing action as launch is unread. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-21
- lease-reason-carry-weak: the lease cases check that the reason carries "lease", which the rule-name prefix always supplies; only "sim-test" pins the reason text (hooks/test_guard.py). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- lease-session-raw-payload: simulator-boot-lease reads the payload's `session_id` directly, not through session_id_of (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py), the home the other call sites use. - sim-fixture-folder-silent: test_guard.py `folder_path` does not check that its fake xcrun folder exists, so a misnamed fixture reads as an unreadable device list and the guard allows. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25

- mutant-lint-copy-cost: each guard mutant copy now holds lint/ (97 files, 2.6 MB, about 9 ms); copies live until the run ends, so peak temp rises from about 0.6 GB to about 1.8 GB (estimate). Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- mutant-watch-tree-lint: the decision-watch and ruling mutant trees in hooks/mutate_guard.py may lack ../lint as the guard copy did; no verdict path to `_layout_module` found, not measured in full. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- route-unread-wrappers: simulator-boot-route and the cap do not read `xcrun xcodebuild` or `arch -arm64 xcodebuild`, and they judge `ssh mac "xcodebuild ..."` against local devices. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- detached-launch-glued-amp: detached-launch passes a `&` glued to a closing quote (`xcodebuild ... "id=U"&`). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- route-remedy-glued-action: simulator-boot-route reads `(cd ios && xcodebuild ... test)` and `test-without-building` as no test action, so the remedy names the generic destination; the verdict is still deny. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- route-glued-fd-redirect: a redirect glued to the destination quote (`'name=iPhone 15'2>&1`) reads as another name, so the boot is not judged. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- sim-test-record-silent: sim-test drops the record call's output and failure, so a failed record leaves its device unrecorded and the sweep keeps it. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- route-build-unmeasured: simulator-boot-route refuses `build`, `clean` and `-showBuildSettings` naming a shut-down simulator; whether those boot one is unmeasured (needs a Mac). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-26
- sim-test-untested-stops: no red test pins the 10 s KILL fallback, the crash window expiring, or the cleanup stop of the child. The HUP trap is pinned by outcome only, since bash 5 runs the EXIT trap on a fatal signal (lint/test_sim_test.sh). Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- sim-test-child-signals: a backgrounded xcodebuild ignores INT and QUIT (no job control), so sim-test stops only xcodebuild itself, with TERM then KILL; its children may outlive it. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-20
- sim-test-late-reports: late reports of an earlier crash loop on the same UDID can stop a healthy run. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-21
- sim-test-kill-skips-cleanup: KILL on sim-test skips its cleanup, so the lock folder and a simulator it booted stay; the next run takes over a dead lock. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- sim-test-rename-back-gap: `take_over` puts a renamed folder back with a second rename; a run that takes the lock between the two renames is not covered (bin/sim-test). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- sim-test-ctrl-c-in-boot: Ctrl-C during `simctl boot` leaves the device booted, since `booted_by_me` is set after the boot returns (bin/sim-test). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- sim-test-lock-per-config-dir: the lock follows CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, so two config folders mean two locks. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- bypass-closer-glued: simulator-boot-bypass passes a shell closer glued to the last word: `(cd x && open -a Simulator)`, `open -a Simulator&`, `open -a Simulator>/dev/null`, and `$(xcrun simctl boot)`. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- bootstatus-flag-boots: `xcrun simctl bootstatus X -b` boots a shut-down device and is not refused. The ruling names neither a refusal nor an exemption for it. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-19
- bypass-rare-false-alarms: simulator-boot-bypass refuses three rare, harmless forms: `open -R .../Simulator.app`, `open -a Safari --args -b com.apple.iphonesimulator`, and a heredoc that writes a script holding `simctl boot`. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- bypass-flag-form-passes: simulator-boot-bypass passes `simctl --set P boot` and a backtick-wrapped boot. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- feel-step-set-done-untried: triage can set a feel step to done with no device trial, and the step then leaves every upkeep row (skills/roadmap/build.py upkeep). Decision: feel-fix-waits-device-check. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- feel-rename-half-similar: a rename under 50% similar, or with diff.renames off, reads as a delete: safe fail, wrong message (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- feel-edit-swap-message: an edit that drops `**Feel.**` and adds a valid device trial fails with a misleading message (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- feel-trial-time-space: a device-trial time with a space instead of `T` fails, though step JSON takes it (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- feel-lint-says-device-check: the lint message says "device check" where prose says "device trial" (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- feel-probe-skips-debt-check: the compliance probe in other repos does not run the debt check (lint/compliance_probes.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- feel-trial-trailing-text: a device-trial line with text after the time (a trailing period, a device name) reads as no trial, and the FAIL text prints no accepted form (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- feel-debt-abandon-blocked: an abandoned feel debt (screen removed, owner drops it) cannot be deleted or have its Feel line dropped; only a false trial line gets past (lint/check_record_slugs.py). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-21
- feel-trial-older-than-fix: the step row never compares a trial's `at` with the landing time, so a later feel PR on a step with an old trial passes (skills/roadmap/build.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- lane-lint-digit-sha-refused: an all-digit short sha is read as a PR number by `merge --lane-lint` and refused, never a false pass (merge/merge.py lane_lint). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- lane-lint-hex-branch-read-as-sha: a local-only branch named like hex (cafe123) is read as a sha by `merge --lane-lint` (merge/merge.py). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- lane-lint-sigterm-leaks-worktree: a SIGTERM between `git worktree add` and the try in lane_lint can leak a locked worktree (merge/merge.py lane_lint). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- lane-lint-stale-origin-ref: after a branch is deleted on origin, a stale origin/<name> makes `merge --lane-lint` lint the old head (merge/merge.py). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- answered-check-case: the ref, hash and path patterns match case, so a capitalised source reads as no source (lint/report_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- answered-check-question-line: a line that ends in "?" is exempt whole, so a claim and a question on one line pass (lint/report_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- answered-check-inline-fence: a line that starts with an inline triple-backtick span opens a fence; "#2" counts as a source; "no-op" counts as a negator (lint/report_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- answered-check-silent-error: main() swallows an answered-check error with no stderr line (lint/report_guard.py).
- ruling-cite-plain-mode-missing-record: ruling_cite plain mode: a reason like "Item 8: decisions/zzz" names a record that does not exist; the refusal stands (hooks/question_form.py ruling_cite). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- report-guard-quote-prefix-indented-code: QUOTE_PREFIX strips 3 spaces, so INDENTED_CODE (4 spaces) never matches; 4-space code with `?` costs one extra model call (lint/report_guard.py QUOTE_PREFIX, INDENTED_CODE). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- report-guard-ask-text-cuts-front: ASK_TEXT_MAX cuts from the front, so an Input Needed item above the last 6000 characters is not shown to the model (lint/report_guard.py ASK_TEXT_MAX). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- fail-open-three-paths: rewrite_relay fails open on a transcript error (lint/report_guard.py rewrite_relay). call_judge's outer except fails open on a past_rulings bug (hooks/call_judge.py). question_form's readline has no timeout. A harness kill at 35 s or 70 s passes silently (hooks/question_form.py readline). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- no-direction-rule-no-candidate-test: NONE_DIRECTION_RULE says the reason names no target. Item 8 says the reason names the listed id. No test covers a re-ask that has candidates. The re-ask cases use PAGE_REPO, which lists none (hooks/test_call_judge.py PAGE_REPO). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- call-judge-unreadable-source-untested: No case reaches the unreadable-source branch of past_ruling_context (hooks/call_judge.py). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- rewrite-stamp-binds-to-sentence: the ruling stamp binds to the sentence, not the clause. "The <stamp A> ruling changed Y, and x was rewritten for the <stamp B> ruling" reads stamp A (lint/report_guard.py _ruling_stamp). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- rewrite-stamp-utc-clock-date: a clock-only UTC or GMT stamp takes the Central date, so the ruling can read a day early, a miss only (lint/report_guard.py _stamp_time). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- rewrite-stamp-recall-forms: "ruling (<stamp>)", "ruling, <stamp>" and "ruling <stamp>" are not read, so the claim passes (lint/report_guard.py _ruling_stamp). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- rewrite-unfetched-lane-refused: a true report about a lane branch that is not fetched is refused until the branch is fetched; cost one turn. Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- pair-title-quotes-short-id: a pull title names every step id it quotes, such as "Revert r5 and c91". So a todo r5 goes red. False-alarm risk; it becomes a debt on a real incident (skills/roadmap/build.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- check-ruling-kind-judge-conflict: check_ruling_kind and the prose judge give conflicting verdicts on a second Kind line. The judge required a Kind line under a new ruling section; the lint fails on two. The judge also refused folding the section under the ruling with one Kind line kept (2026-11-01 21:40 CST). Decision: test-practices, PR 1028. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-25
- cc-facts-word-in-string: cc_facts reads an env var name from `word.NAME` inside a string literal as a code read (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- cc-facts-unbounded-decl: `_cc_assigned` finds a declaration at any distance, so a lost literal can resolve to an unrelated same-named number and miss a change (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). A bound of about 50 KB before and 2 KB after would close it. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- cc-line-cut-tail: `_cc_line` cuts a 240-char line, so a one-fact change with a long list value loses the "Read:" pointer (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- window-env-forms-unread: `window_limit` takes the env window only as plain digits or with a k or m suffix. A form Claude Code reads, such as `1e6`, falls back to the settings or default window (500k). The hook then nudges later than Claude Code compacts when the real window is below 500k. It nudges earlier when the window is above (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- last-nudge-gap-no-summary: with no early summary in Claude Code and no percent override, the last nudge can sit ~130k tokens before Claude Code's hard trigger (500k, f 0.2). Owner ruling 2026-11-01 19:27 CST is in precompact-handoff item 2. Decision: precompact-handoff. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- grant-unfenced-paste: a pasted, unfenced message with a grant word and an act or scope word, such as "approved ... all checks", fires the grant check; a `[note] -> not-a-grant` item clears it (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- answer-own-options: a reply such as "2 b" that answers the session's own options, in a repo with a waiting page, needs a send to the page writer (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- grant-no-word-miss: a grant with no grant word, such as "you can merge anything green" or "ok to merge", is not caught (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- answer-in-prose-miss: a chat answer to a page item with other text after it, such as "143 b, ok" or "143 b!", is not caught (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- grant-home-not-read: a grant block passes with any tracked ruling home, and the guard does not read the record. An unclosed inline fence hides the rest of the message from the grant scan (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- grant-root-home-any: in any block with a grant word and an act word, `settings.json` and `.claude/CLAUDE.md` pass as ruling homes, even when the grant match is false (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- closed-token-midline: a bare `[closed]` token in mid-line, after the home, stops the standing, page and compaction open-id checks for that item (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- grant-scope-misses: "all repositories", "all-repos", "all the repos" and "every repository" are not scope words. A negated "you do not have approval to push" fires the check (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- question-placeholder-trailing-period: a Goal, Problem or Based on line that is a placeholder followed by a period passes, because the check is a fullmatch of one `<...>` (hooks/question_form.py is_placeholder). Decision: question-form. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- port-str-replace-no-symbol: a `context` str_replace on a port item skips the file:symbol rule, because the stored port flag is unseen there (hooks/question_form.py form_str_replace_hit). Decision: port-keeps-behaviour. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- departs-bare-angle-name: "Departs from: main — adds <Modal>" is refused as a placeholder, though `<Modal>` can be a real component name; "adds a <Modal>" passes (hooks/question_form.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- unread-early-line-east-blind: the early +05:00 line in the unread test can fail only on a clock at or west of UTC+3; CI on UTC covers it (hooks/test_decision_watch.py, mods/decision-watch/decision-watch.test.mjs). Step: guard-log-time-offset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- grant-no-word-miss: a grant with no grant word, such as "you can merge anything green" or "ok to merge", is not caught (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- guard-log-between-untested: `--between` in main() has no test, only window(); a `Z` bound fails before Python 3.11, so a pace read passes `+00:00` (lint/guard_log_report.py). Step: guard-log-time-offset.
- decision-watch-long-comment: a comment line near hooks/decision_watch.py:875 runs to 154 characters. Cosmetic. Step: guard-log-time-offset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- undo-presence-skip: a line the merge-base holds, which both parents keep and the merge drops, reads as kept, in place or across a rename, by the `in_b` skip in lost_in_parent (lint/check_silent_undo.py). Decision: silent-undo-check-design Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- undo-readd-old-name: main renames a file and adds a new file at the old name. Git pairs no rename, so lost_in_parent reads the base at the new name and flags a line the branch edited (lint/check_silent_undo.py). False alarm, same on main. Decision: silent-undo-check-design Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- report-guard-model-window: the report guard's model-name check flagged a correct "reviewer haiku" twice on 2026-11-01, in a lane where the round-1 reviewer ran haiku and later rounds ran sonnet. It matched only the newest reviewer spawn. spawn_models keeps a set per role, so the transcript window it reads likely leaves out the older spawn; cause not traced (lint/report_guard.py spawn_models). Decision: none
- handoff-unread: --post-compact in hooks/reread_check.py sets the flag with no agent_id check. Unmeasured whether PostCompact fires inside an agent; if it does, the main session is refused until it reads its handoff. Decision: precompact-handoff Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- handoff-unread: a session with no handoff at compaction writes one, then its next guarded call is refused until it reads that file. One Read. Decision: precompact-handoff Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- page-locked-card-rebuild: a card built locked with its fold closed rebuilds once more on the next render, and a push in that render moves the caret in a note being typed (skills/waiting-page/page.html buildCard sets state.unfold after the signature is stored). Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- page-unstood-draft-locks: an unpicked stood pick leaves draft `{unstood:true}`, so the fold stays locked and reads "Stays open: your answer is ready" though Send has nothing for the item; too strict, hides nothing. Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- page-seen-divider-words: the seen divider's words were "Asked before, not answered" in the mockup (docs/design/stale-asks-sink.html); the page says "Asked before" (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- lane-create-session-refused: a test-author in lane merge-lock-record-drift called create_session by mistake (its brief forbids it); auto mode refused it, correctly. The report guard then asks the owner to allow the tool, a false alarm when the refusal was right (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: none.
- shallow-probe-fails-allows: if `rev-parse --is-shallow-repository` fails (git older than 2.15, or a 10 s timeout), `branch_is_ancestor` gives None, and the guard allows `branch -D` over an only copy with a subject-unread note; main denies it. Only an environment fault reaches this, not a Claude act (`hooks/git_reads.py` `is_shallow`). Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- shallow-only-copy-wording: in a shallow clone, the branch-delete refusal and the sweep's only-copy reason say "not an ancestor" or "only copy" when ancestry is unproven. The refusal fails closed, and the archive remedy loses nothing. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ste-changed-none-says-shallow: `bin/ste-changed` names "shallow clone" as the cause for every unknown ancestry, even a 10 s timeout. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- sweep-shallow-skips-cherry: in a shallow clone, the worktree merged read in `janitor/sweep.py` does not see a tree merged by patch, so the sweep keeps it until `git fetch --unshallow` runs. The error is on the safe side. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- shallow-bash-sites: in a shallow clone, four sites print false lines: `githooks/main-ref/reference-transaction` (exit 1 reads as not on origin), `install.sh` (logs "a commit REF does not hold"), the session-start hook in `settings.json` ("did not fast-forward"), and `hooks/session_start.sh` (the "N behind" count can be too high). Each fails closed or is advisory only. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- is-shallow-no-mutant: `is_shallow` has no mutant, because the git_reads shallow case loads `git_reads.py` from its own folder, not from the tree under test. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ask-send-position-untested: no test pins where Send ask sits or that Copy ask stays hidden after Undo. The over-limit tests accept either a disabled Send ask or an enabled one with a notice (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs). Step: page-ask-box (the waiting-page Ask box). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ask-term-no-glossary-row: GLOSSARY.md has no row for "ask" (the owner's free text on the waiting page), and the guard rows use "ask" for a permission ask. Step: page-ask-box (the waiting-page Ask box). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-alarm-greener-conclusions: alarm.sh reds only failure, timed_out and startup_failure (actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh). merge.py base_state also reds action_required, stale and any other conclusion but success, skipped, neutral and cancelled (merge/merge.py), so the alarm reads greener than the merge brake. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-alarm-run-window: alarm.sh reads 100 runs (`--limit 100`, actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh) and merge.py base_state reads 50 (`per_page: 50`, merge/merge.py). On a busy repo an old red schedule run can fall off the page and go unjudged. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-alarm-same-second-tie: merge/merge.py base_state judges "newer" by list position, actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh by strict createdAt. On a same-second tie the merge tool can clear a red run and the alarm opens an issue, so the alarm is the stricter side. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-alarm-unknown-is-quiet: a failed alarm read or issue write prints `unknown` and a `::warning::` only, and exits 0 (actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh, actions/ci-alarm/issue.sh), so a mis-permissioned or failing alarm opens no issue and no red job. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-cancelled-red-run-cleared: a cancelled run with a red job (one job failed, then the run was cancelled) is red. A newer success run that passed every job name can clear it across groups, as it clears a failed run. actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh never judges cancelled runs, so only the merge tool sees it (merge/merge.py base_state). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- ci-clear-compares-job-names: the cross-group clear in merge/merge.py base_state and actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh compares job names and conclusions, not steps. A same-named job that skips a step in one event could clear the other event's failed run. Not reachable in this repo's checks.yml; other repos unmeasured. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-19
- verdict-skip-other-runners: TAP `# skip`, go `--- SKIP:`, mocha `pending` and cargo `ignored` read PASS in `bin/verdict`; no runner in the repos uses them. Plan row S8. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- verdict-skip-stray-text: any exit-0 output that reads `N skipped` or `skipped=N`, such as a sync log, shows SKIP. Display only; the exit code is unchanged. Plan row S8. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- caution-line-missing: session start prints no caution line, not even the unknown line, when the claude-settings checkout ($d) is unset or has no lint/layout.py (hooks/session_start.sh). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- review-cost-gh-only: `--reviewed`'s cost counts gh calls only. It also runs `git fetch origin main` and local reads, and judges every PR on main's required checks. The pinned-list five-call count has no test. Decision: pr-review-record. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- design-round-alone-done: `--round 1:design-reviewer:...:PASS` alone reads done with no code reviewer. That is a lane claim. Decision: pr-review-record. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- report-guard-url-string-compare: lint/report_guard.py (about :591) compares the page-write URL as a string. It should call layout.is_waiting_page_url (lint/layout.py), the one home of "same artifact". A /code/artifact/<id> form or a URL with a query string blocks wrongly. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- send-error-still-counts: on the transcript path, a send whose result is an error still counts (read lint/_transcript.py send_targets; main behaviour). The page-writer key path of lint/report_guard.py `question_problem` excludes a send whose result failed or is not delivered (read lint/report_guard.py question_problem; lint/_transcript.py delivered_tool_use_ids). `failed_tool_use_ids` in lint/_transcript.py already reads such results for hooks/feedback_guard.py (read hooks/feedback_guard.py failed_tool_use_ids). The cloud form of fromSession for ack matching (read lint/_transcript.py peer_sender) is unmeasured. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- waiting-page-chat-first-heading: skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md keeps the heading "Chat first" above same-turn text. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- waiting-page-chat-first-heading: skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md keeps the heading "Chat first" (about :178) above same-turn text. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- report-guard-shared-names-eager: lint/report_guard.py imports shared_names eagerly (:152), so a broken install missing hooks/shared_names.py skips the Stop check (fail open). install.sh lands hooks flat, so only a broken install hits it. Fix: none — rare and harmless (only a broken install that lacks the hooks file hits it; install.sh lands hooks flat). Until 2026-11-21.
- layout-git-reads-lazy-import: lint/layout.py repo_name imports hooks/git_reads.py on first use (~lines 114-120, 158) outside its try, so a broken install missing git_reads.py makes repo_name raise ImportError where main returned None; callers include path(). install.sh lands hooks flat, so only a broken install hits it. Fix: none — rare and harmless (only a broken install that lacks the hooks file hits it; install.sh lands hooks flat). Until 2026-11-21.
- cite-reader-module-alias: `from <pkg> import merge as m`, then `m.name`, is not read. A helper read that way still counts as a helper, and an outside call is not scanned (lint/test_rule_audit.py _names_read_outside). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- cite-reader-rebound-catch: rebinding the caught name inside a Stop handler (`e = OSError(..)`, then `fail(str(e))`) still reads as cited (lint/test_rule_audit.py _caught_as_stop). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- cite-reader-stop-variable: `err = Stop("x"); raise err` is not read, and the `return False, "..."` tuples in wait_checks are not read, so their cite text is unchecked (lint/test_rule_audit.py; merge/merge.py wait_checks). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- merge-network-stops-uncited: the network-error stops in merge/merge.py (about lines 666, 1937, 2315) stay exempt from the cite check. Decision standing-merge-grant (the standing merge grant) covers them: the merge tool "retries a network error 3 times". Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- merge-ignorechecks-no-record: the `merge.ignoreChecks` stop in required_names (merge/merge.py, about line 801) is exempt, because no decision states that a required check is never ignored. That rule is in actions/stamp/README.md only. Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-20
- rejected-git-reads-absent: `_read_steps` in hooks/question_form.py maps git's "not a git repository" to an absent roadmap, on both the normal path and the early-exit path. The pre-check proves only that a `.git` entry exists, so a broken worktree pointer or a GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES setting skips the step-note search with only a stderr unknown line. This is rare, and Claude does not set these in normal work. Lane ruling, 2026-11-01 23:25 CST: keep absent, so a broken worktree does not refuse every question. Decision: question-form Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- git-stderr-locale: the "not a git repository" match is English-only. A translated git message reads as unreadable, the safe side. Fix if it ever fires: set LC_ALL=C in the git child's environment. Decision: question-form Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- shell-named-file-header: `_INTERPRETER_FIRST` reads a file or folder named like a shell (`cat > start.sh <<'EOF'`, `docs/sh/x.md`) as a shell header, so the body stays in view. Where: hooks/guard_core.py. Decision: env-file-stays-unread (no session reads or writes an env file's contents; a quoted heredoc body is text). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- odd-backtick-unquoted-body: an odd backtick in an unquoted heredoc body makes every layer read the rest of the body as a command. Where: hooks/guard_core.py strip_heredoc_bodies. Decision: env-file-stays-unread (no session reads or writes an env file's contents; a quoted heredoc body is text). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- written-file-run-forms-unread: `make -f f`, `at -f f`, `mv f g; bash g`, `bash ${f}` are not read as runs of a written file. A body holding a nested interpreter heredoc, which main refused by accident, now passes in these forms. Where: hooks/guard_core.py. Decision: guard-guards-mistakes-only (a guard stops Claude's mistakes, not an attacker's forms). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- pin-lint-named-file-silent: lint/check_agent_models.py ~121 says an unreadable record is reported. That report runs only with no argument (~141). A run on named files passes a mismatched pin silently. CI runs the full form (.github/workflows/checks.yml ~407). Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- pin-lint-key-case: the pin reader (lint/check_agent_models.py ~66-78) reads `model:` case-sensitively. haiku_model (~49-54) does not. So `Model: sonnet` on reviewer passes. A quoted `name: "reviewer"` is linted, but hooks/guard.py role_has_model_pin compares the raw value. Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- pin-lint-test-gaps: no unknown-family pin case (for example, `fable`) in lint/test_check_agent_models.py. No block-scalar `model:` case in hooks/test_guard.py role-model family G. The lint test has one (test_pin_is_read_past_comments_quotes_and_indented_text). frontmatter_keys now skips indented lines (hooks/guard.py ~2045). Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- other-checkout-agent-flag: `has_agent` in `judge` reads only `agent_id` (hooks/guard.py, the `other_checkout_hit` call), while the agent flag passed to `judge_shell` (hooks/guard.py, ~3630) reads `agent_id` or `agent_type`. No measured payload differs, since agents carry both (hooks/guard.py:785). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- cloud-disk-full: with the brake off in a cloud session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE=true`), a cloud lane can use up its disk allowance. Writes then fail with "no space left on device". Decision: machine-load-brake (the machine-load brake). Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-20
- override-trailing-mark-on-word: `override off.`, `override 15m.`, `override ,15m` and `override -off` grant a one-act override, not off or timed. The whole-prompt check matches only a bare word. The owner may type a trailing period after `off` and keep an override meant to end (hooks/override.py). Same on main. Decision: owner-override Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-20
- base-copies-delete-refused: a staged or unstaged delete of a file whose content sits in pushed HEAD keeps `git worktree remove` refused, though nothing is lost (hooks/git_reads.py dirty_paths_are_pushed_copies). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- base-copies-old-ancestor-refused: An index matches a pushed ancestor outside HEAD, its merge-base and HEAD's last 20 reflog entries. That match keeps the remove refused. The candidate set is bounded by design, with no history walk (hooks/git_reads.py). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- ready-heal-second-run: a ready head may already hold its page. If the base gains a record during the wait, the page conflicts. prove() then heals and pushes a merge commit after green, so CI runs twice. The batch brake keeps the base still during a batch run (review round 1 of PR #1035, read from code). Decision: records-index-at-merge Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-20
- ready-resumed-claim-page-push: a resumed claim head with a stale page still pushes a page-only commit to a ready PR, since the draft step refuses a claim head (merge/merge.py confirm; PR #1035). Decision: records-index-at-merge Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- claim-remedy-wording-narrow: the claim-head remedy says the base "claims nothing for" the claim; it also fires when the base has kinds but the claim made no numbers. The remedy is still right (review round 2 of PR #1035). Decision: records-index-at-merge Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- ruling-kind-judge-live-unmeasured: the Edit judge's refusal of an added second ruling section is pinned as prompt text only, with the model stubbed. Whether the live model refuses it, or also refuses a legitimate added subsection, is unmeasured. Decision: rulings-declare-kind Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- ruling-kind-first-section-stub: an Edit that adds the first ruling section, with no Kind line, to a record with no Kind line passes the judge. The lint catches it at pull request time. Decision: rulings-declare-kind Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- ruling-kind-lint-no-remedy: the lint's "found N" message for two Kind lines does not name the remedy, a second ruling's own record. Only the judge and the record name it. Decision: rulings-declare-kind Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- auto-mode-refuses-page-grant: auto mode refused `mcp__claude-code-remote__send_message` from the coordinator (Nov 1 23:05 CST) as self-modification, on a brief for a lane that adds an auto-mode allow line. The owner had granted it by a Queue page save (item 149 b), which auto mode does not count as his words. A page grant that widens auto mode needs his words typed in chat. Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- red-run-rebase-judges-old-commits: a lane rebased onto main after the check landed has each of its commits judged, so a commit from before the check needs a trailer after the rebase. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- red-run-sha-not-verified: the old-code sha in a Red-run trailer is never checked to exist, so a made-up sha passes (lint/check_red_run.py). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- red-run-existing-behaviour-claims-none: a test of existing behaviour fails on no old code, so it can only claim `Red-run: none: <reason>` (lint/check_red_run.py). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- test-temp-sweep-unproven: no test proves the sweep in lint/_test_temp.py, which removes run folders older than a day; the red-run suite sweep case was dropped. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- red-run-merge-revert-judged: the revert skip misses a merge revert. Git writes `This reverts commit <sha>, reversing`, and REVERT (lint/check_red_run.py:35) needs a `.` or line end after the sha, so the revert is judged and needs a trailer. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- red-run-base-unknown-quiet: `--base origin/main` in a repo whose default branch is not main gives UNKNOWN with no git reason. commits() runs `rev-parse --quiet` (lint/check_red_run.py:63), so git() gets empty stderr (:48). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- red-run-fixture-data-needs-none: a commit that adds only fixture data under `test_support`, `testdata` or `__snapshots__` is a test path (TEST_DIRS, hooks/guard.py:1383, read by is_test_rel), so it needs `Red-run: none: <reason>`. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- merge-superseded-cancel-only: a cancel row of a superseded cancelled run drops with no newest-run row of that name. Then head_read reads green where main read red. Needs one workflow with different job sets on one head. Fix: none — no incident, and the fix would stop dropping skip and pending rows with no newest-run match, which risks new false holds (merge/merge.py Host.superseded).
- merge-superseded-two-run-reads: a head with two or more linked runs reads `gh run list` twice per pass (Host.superseded, then judge_runs); the check-runs read and the runs read are not atomic. Cost only (merge/merge.py Host.checks). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- merge-cancelled-newest-twin: a cancelled newest run beside an older pass of the same name reads green, by main's twin rule (Host.classify, Host.judge_runs). Unchanged by merge-ignores-superseded-runs. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- silent-write-ref-gaps: guard rule silent-write reads seven git subcommands, a `src:dst` fetch, `gh pr merge` and `make merge`; a silenced `git branch -D` or `git update-ref -d` passes. Widen only after an incident (rule building-guard-needs-incident). Plan: refusal-vs-rule. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- silent-write-comment-stale: the comment above SILENT_WRITE_SUBCOMMANDS in hooks/guard.py quotes a rule text a cull cut and says six subcommands where the constant holds seven. Fix in a comment-only lane.
- load-brake-skips-create-session: guard rule load-brake does not read `create_session`. A cloud session runs in its own container and adds no load here. Test that claim if a local environment id is ever used. Decision: machine-load-brake. Fix: none — needs the Mac. Until: 2026-11-21
- pwd-delete-trailing-quote: `rm -rf "$(pwd)"/*` followed by a comment that holds a lone quote passes, though the same delete of `./*` is refused; RM_PARENT_RAW has no `$(pwd)` form (hooks/guard_push_delete.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- checkout-f-cluster: `git checkout -fb x` passes while `git switch -fc x` is read as a force; checkout reads -f exactly and switch reads clusters (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- switch-clean-cluster-false-alarm: `git switch -Cf x` asks or denies, and `git clean -fdn` or `-nfd` in a lost primary is refused, though a dry run deletes nothing (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py).
- merge-liftable-worktree-gone: the worktree-gone branch of fail() in merge/merge.py raises a plain Stop, so a liftable stop that reaches it prints its flag and also "No override lifts this refusal". It needs the tree removed after the claim push plus an unreadable-history stop. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- merge-silent-undo-no-repair: on 2026-11-02 at 00:53 CST the auto-mode classifier refused `Bash` for a `git push --force-with-lease` that would replace a lane's own merge commit (Git Destructive). A later commit cannot clear check_silent_undo, which reads the merge commit itself. The lane moved to a new branch and PR (#1068 replaces #1021). That is the cost of a silent-undo failure on a merge commit. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- press-label-verb-only: the press-link label excusal reads the verb only, so label "Approve on GitHub" beside "The session approves PR #5." passes. Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21
- shell-line-no-arg-chain: command-in-item passes a chain of commands with no arguments (`whoami; hostname`, `pwd && whoami`, `date | sort`). Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- shell-line-capital-prose: command-in-item lowercases the command word, so capitalised prose with `;` (`Option A; Option B`) is refused, and the decision does not say so. Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-nested-def-missed: the time-pinned finder keeps Python taint inside one function, so a nested def that asserts on a name tainted in module or outer scope is not listed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-mock-now-alarm: the time-pinned finder lists `mock.assert_called_once_with(now)` with a clock-tainted `now` as elapsed-assert, though it checks an injected clock, not speed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-destructure-decl: JS and Swift destructured declarations never taint a name. `const { ms } = planted(); assert.ok(ms < 10000)` is not listed (lint/find_time_pinned.py; real case: q_max harness/tests/run-tests.test.mjs, about lines 592-596). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-bound-duration-arg: a duration bound to a name and then passed to a helper is not listed: `const dt = Date.now() - t0; expect(toMs(dt))`. Only the inline spelling is read (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-helper-clock-diff: a Python helper at module level, or a method helper such as `self.elapsed()`, that returns a clock difference does not taint a test's assert (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-literal-hides-ms: an object literal inside an asserted call hides a duration: `expect(report({ ms: Date.now() - t0 }))` is not listed. The finder does not read a clock past a `{` in a call's arguments (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-swift-since-helper: a Swift `func ms(since:)` called with a bare start time is not listed. A Swift `func` taints nothing, and a clock passed to a call is only an input (degen FreeSpaceCountSafetyTests.swift lines 188 and 213; lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-offset-clock-input: an injected clock with an offset is listed as elapsed-assert: `edit_idle(tree, time.monotonic() - 1)` inside `assertIsNone` (claude-settings janitor/test_sweep.py:5178). The finder reads a `-` inside a call's arguments as a measured duration, though the test sets a clock, not a speed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- time-pinned-bracket-scan-slow: each hit can start a bracket scan across much of the file, so a multi-MB file with many hits is slow: a mutated 4.4 MB file took 12 s. Real files are unaffected (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- pkill-inverse-parent: `pkill -v -P $$` and `pkill --inverse -P $$` pass the kill guard, though they kill every process that is not the shell's child. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- lsof-kill-in-quoted-string: `lsof -t | xargs -I{} sh -c "kill {}"`, and `xargs` into `python3 -c`, `perl -e`, `node -e` or `busybox kill`, pass, because no plain kill word follows. Main refused them. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- nohup-prefix-setsid: `nohup env A=1 setsid cmd`, `nohup nice setsid cmd` and `nohup sudo setsid cmd` pass, because the detached check reads past `nohup` only. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- impeccable-verbs-off-table: after `;`, `doctor`, `hooks on`, `teach`, `pin`, `unpin`, a capitalised verb and `then,` pass, because only Commands table rows count. Decision: design-skills Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- builder-test-path-symlink: the builder test-path check uses normpath, not realpath, so a symlink from outside a repo into its test folder passes. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- lsof-kill-glued-paren: `(lsof -ti :3000 | xargs kill)` passes, because the kill check reads `kill)` as one word. Main refused it. The same gap holds for pgrep on main. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- spec-prose-oracle: a builder write to a prose fixture under spec/ (`spec/golden/out.md`, `spec/fixtures/expected.txt`) passes, though such a file is a test oracle. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- author-raw-cwd: the test-author and designer branch of role_write_hit reads a `/c/` cwd raw, so on Windows an author write outside its scope passes. Same on main. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests Fix: none — needs a Windows run. Until: 2026-11-22
- auto-mode-refuses-run-cancel: auto mode refused `Bash` (Nov 1 22:57 CST) for `gh api -X POST .../actions/runs/41377928751/cancel` on q_max, as "Interfere With Workloads". The run was a browser tier hung toward its 180-minute cap. MCP `cancel_workflow_run` on a session's own run passed with no refusal (Nov 6); a cancel of another session's run via MCP is unmeasured, and stays that session's act (shared-trees-no-machine-wide-kill). Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts (route run-cancel-mcp-cloud) Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-22
- bounded-git-kill-leaves-git: the bound's kill stops the subshell, not git under it, so a killed fetch runs on after the hook exits; on Windows CI it held FETCH_HEAD past cleanup (main run 41418810686, caseG1). Harm seen: none beyond a busy temp file (hooks/session_start.sh bounded_git). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- builder-diff-check-fail-open: On the batch-64b local run (2026-11-02, batch runner 7), hooks/test_guard.py failed 48 builder-refuse cases, all "got allow". Lint, test_merge and test_install_src ran in parallel from the same worktree. The failure did not reproduce alone, or on main commit 07f855dc with the same heads. Suspect: diff_violation in hooks/guard.py returns unread, and the commit is allowed, when its git read fails (fail open). Unmeasured. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests). Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- qmax-select-exits-1: q_max `select` (`harness/tests-for.mjs`) exited 1 in 24 scored PRs, so every changed suite ran there. A q_max commit that lacks `harness/tests-for.mjs` now reads the HEAD copy. Cause of the exit 1 unread (`_select_suites`, hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: step:notes-reverify-flags
- banchi-cli-identify-red-clean: banchi `t7/cli_identify.py` was not green on the clean source (assert) in PRs 898, 923, 926, 930 and 956. Cause unread. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- defines-checks-misses-augassign: `defines_checks` reads only a top-level `CHECKS =` or annotated `CHECKS:`, so `CHECKS +=` and other forms are missed (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-22
- t7-box-map-no-run: `harness/tests/t7_box_map.py` matches the tN regex but has no `run()`, so it reads crash (hooks/mutate_guard.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- t7-group-cap-margin: a T7 group takes 94 s against the 120 s suite cap (`--suite-timeout` default, hooks/mutate_guard.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- playwright-closed-title-crash: `_PW_NOT_ASSERT` reads "has been closed" in a test title as a crash, which undercounts kills (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- pr-budget-restarts-per-part: `--pr-budget` restarts for each banchi profile part, since `replay_pr` counts from its own start (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- budget-s-flake-margin: `BUDGET_S = 5.0` leaves about 1 s above the first mutant's start, so a slow machine can flake (hooks/test_mutate_replay.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- mutate-js-linecount-per-site: `lineOf` in hooks/mutate_js.mjs splits the source from its start for each site, so the cost is O(n) per site. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- mutate-js-multiline-site-first-line: a multi-line site is keyed by its first line (`lineOf(node)`, hooks/mutate_js.mjs). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- renamed-file-all-lines-changed: `changed_lines` runs with `--no-renames`, so a renamed file reads as all lines added (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- lane-force-push-refused: the auto-mode classifier refused a lane's `git push --force-with-lease` to its own draft branch, to add Red-run trailers, twice on 2026-11-02: lane worktree-base-copies-remove at 00:30 CST (replacement PR #1064) and lane tci-6 at 06:05 CST (replacement PR #1087). - stale-cancel-all-skipped: a cancelled main run whose jobs were all skipped clears when a newer covering run holds those job names, whatever their conclusions (`cleared`, merge/merge.py). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- stale-cancel-missing-head-sha: the cancelled-run clear treats a newer run with no `head_sha` as on another head; GitHub always sends it (`cleared`, merge/merge.py). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- rewrite-shared-try-drops-all: lint/report_guard.py rewrite_problems runs answered_problem, recovered_problem, the models check and the unstarted check in one try. One raise drops all four reasons with no log. The landed and unlanded paths isolate each call. Unlikely in normal work: it needs a malformed transcript record. Fix: none — rare and harmless. - rewrite-no-marker-skips-judge: lint/report_guard.py, the stop_hook_active rewrite path. A rewrite after a refusal that only the shape check made carries no marker, so the answered and Recovered checks do not judge it again. lint/test_report_guard.py pins this. It may be stale; the owner has not ruled. Unlikely in normal work: it needs a shape-only refusal. Decision: none. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-23
- relay-comment-stale-in-main: lint/report_guard.py, the comment above `reasons += relay` in main(). It names only the pick and Departs checks. The relay also carries past-ruling, question-keys and answered. Unlikely in normal work: a comment only, no run changes. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- cross-pr-merge-review-partial: the cross-PR merge resolution for PR #1016 and PR #1023 (answered-needs-source) was reviewed only for dropped or doubled checks (reviewer haiku, 2026-11-02). It was not a full lane review. No single file (merge resolution). Unlikely in normal work: the review covered dropped and doubled checks.
- interrupt-tool-refused: a test-author agent called interrupt_session by mistake, and the auto-mode classifier refused it, with no effect. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule).
- page-button-this-item: "this item" in the shared page-button list means "Click this item on GitHub." needs no link, though main refused it. Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- clause-start-noun-verb: a clause after a new break that starts with the noun "merge", "hit" or "approve" ("…, but merge stays manual.") is refused, though main allowed it. Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- close-paren-no-break: ")" is no clause break, so "(The session reruns CI) Mark the PR ready." with no link passes. Decision: act-in-place Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- classifier-refused-read-probe: on 2026-11-02 the auto-mode classifier refused a reviewer's read-only Bash probe of merge/merge.py. The reviewer read the code instead. The coordinator ruled no new allow.
- other-clone-project-dir-unmeasured: the stranded-cwd part of `other-checkout` reads the home clone from `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`; that hooks receive it is read from the Claude Code hooks docs, not measured live. Unset, the part never fires (hooks/guard.py `_clone_scope`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- other-clone-stranded-false-alarm: from a cwd stranded in a separate clone, `cp a <home>/`, `mv`, `sed -i 's/a/b/' <home file>` and `chmod +x <home file>` are refused even when the target is home. The write scan reads sources and non-path words as paths in the stranded clone (hooks/guard.py `elsewhere`). `cd <home> && <cmd>` works. Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- other-clone-silent-catch: `_clone_scope` catches every exception as "not stranded" with no log line, so a bug there turns the stranded part off silently. `_origin_key` reads `github.com/o/r` and `gitlab.com/o/r`, or two relative-path origins, as one repo (hooks/guard.py). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- npm-prefix-subexpr: `npm --prefix (Get-Location)` and `npm --prefix %CD%` read the value as a literal folder name, so a linked cwd is not walked (hooks/guard_push_delete.py npm_prefix_flag). Fix: none — rare form, and the review rated the harm low. Until: 2026-11-21
- retired-advisor-reader: the retired terminal advisor still names a log reader (hooks/reply_judge.py:31, docs/decisions/reply-judge-samples-never-refuses.md:8). The log has no live reader. Fix: none — owner decides. Until: 2026-11-23
- restates-na-by-copied-anchors: a child repo whose rule files hold at least 90% (`lint/compliance_probes.py: NA_SHARE = 0.9`) of the live claude-settings anchors reads as claude-settings, so restates gives n/a there. Reachable only by copying the global rules whole. Decision: child-cites-not-copies (a child repo names a claude-settings record and never copies it). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- restates-kind-line-unit-dropped: a unit whose first line is a `Kind:`, `Plan:` or `Home:` line is dropped whole (`plain_words`, lint/compliance_probes.py), so a copy on the next line with no blank line between passes. Rare in a CLAUDE.md. Decision: child-cites-not-copies (a child repo names a claude-settings record and never copies it). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- page-stale-title-space: `stale_pages.carries_data` renames the live title before it collapses white space, so an untouched page titled "Queue " (with a trailing space, a newline or NBSP) and empty blocks passes `page-stale`. A copy of page.html has the exact title, so normal work does not hit it. Decision: page-stale-publish Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-23
- page-stale-old-main: until a checkout fetches this merge, its origin/main template is still titled "Queue", and an untouched "Queue" page passes `page-stale`. It ends at the fetch. Decision: page-stale-publish Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- merge-background-refused: auto mode's classifier refuses `~/.claude/bin/merge <n> --confirm` run with run_in_background (Auto-Mode Bypass, measured 2026-11-03 16:56 CST); the same command in the foreground matches the allow rule. A merge runs in the foreground. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- merge-confirm-forms-unjudged: `repo_proof_hit` (hooks/guard_merge.py) judges a merge --confirm inside a subshell paren, `bash -c`, `sh -c` or `eval` string like a bare one (`_unproved_confirm`), and cases cover each. It still does not judge a bare `python3 merge.py` run from inside merge/. The allow rule does not cover that form, so the classifier judges it. `timeout` and `pushd` are judged. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- cd-subst-reverts-dir: `cd $(…)` moves the dir back at its `)` in `dir_before_segment` (hooks/guard_core.py), so `cd $(x) && merge N --confirm` is judged in the dir before the cd. All its callers read it this way. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- cd-or-chain-both-taken: `cd B || cd A; merge N --confirm` takes both cds, so the merge is judged in A, though the shell stays in B when the first cd works. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- repo-dir-var-unexpanded: `-C "$HOME/x"` is not expanded (only `~` is), so the merge is denied. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- merge-dev-flag-stripped: merge/launch.py strips `--dev`, so `merge --dev N --confirm` is refused in the cloud. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- launch-checkout-read-as-pr: `python3 merge/launch.py <checkout> N --confirm` parses the checkout as the PR number (merge/launch.py). Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- job-workflow-unreadable-skipped: `job_workflow` (lint/workflow_steps.py) skips an unreadable workflow file with no log. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- ci-job-discovery-no-case: lint/test_ci_job.py has no case for default workflow discovery with 0 or 2 files. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- merge-env-c-allowed: `env -C <dir> merge N --confirm` is allowed in a cloud session, because `dir_before_segment` never applies env's `-C` (hooks/guard_core.py), so the merge is judged in the shell's directory. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- merge-python-w-script: `python3 -W ignore merge/merge.py ...` reads `ignore` as the script, so `merge_tool_args` does not see a merge tool call and the call is not judged (hooks/guard_merge.py `merge_tool_args`). Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- merge-in-substitution-after-cd: a merge inside `$(...)` or backticks is not judged by the merge-confirm-repo clause (hooks/guard_merge.py `_unproved_confirm`). Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- cd-subst-relative-repo-dir: after `cd $(…)`, a relative `-C` resolves against the folder before the cd. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- redirect-strip-quote-blind: `REDIRECTION.sub` still strips redirections without reading quotes in three readers. They are `git_calls` (hooks/guard_core.py), the checkout/restore parse check (hooks/guard.py) and the shared-tree parse check (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py). There a quoted `>` or `<` is cut as a redirection. `strip_redirections` (hooks/guard_core.py) is the quote-aware reader. Decision: merge-confirm-repo
- heredoc-body-scan-other-layers: the default strip_heredoc_bodies keeps every heredoc body when the command holds any interpreter header. So callers that do not pass per_body read data bodies as shell: lane, kill and merge rules in guard.py, ci_wake and decision_watch. Env, cap and frozen read per body. Where: hooks/guard_core.py strip_heredoc_bodies. Decision: env-file-stays-unread
- cap-python-open-write: `python3 -c 'open(".claude/settings.json","w").write(...)'` with the cap key is allowed; the cap rule reads no file write made by interpreter code. Where: hooks/guard.py rule 8. Decision: agent-model-cap
- script-spots-perl-ruby-aliases: perl and ruby shell calls the spot list misses (`*s=\&CORE::system`, `open(P,"| cmd")`, `IPC::Open3`, ruby `send(:system)`, `Process.spawn`, `FileUtils`) read as data in an interpreter body. Where: hooks/guard_env.py _SPOT_CALLS. Decision: agent-model-cap
- script-spots-third-party-shell: python `pexpect`, `sh`, `plumbum`, `fabric`, `timeit` and node `shelljs`, `execa` read as data, where main read the body as shell. Where: hooks/guard_env.py script_spots. Decision: agent-model-cap
- inline-flag-of-script: `python3 x.py -c '...'` and `node app.js -e '...'` read the script's own flag as an inline script, so its text is data. Where: hooks/guard_frozen.py _inline_script_at. Decision: agent-model-cap
- spot-reader-acting-module-alarm: a script that imports an acting module for a non-shell call (`os.getcwd`) is read whole. If it names a frozen path or the cap in a string, `cat <<'X' | python3 -` asks or denies where main allowed it. A bun script importing child_process does the same. Where: hooks/guard_frozen.py py_spots, hooks/guard_env.py script_spots. Decision: agent-model-cap
- auto-mode-refuses-probe-agent: auto mode refused the `Agent` spawn of the hook-allow probe builder (2026-11-03 22:18 CST) as "[Auto-Mode Bypass]", though the owner answered page item 222 with a at 22:16 CST. A page save is not owner words to the classifier (decision grant-reaches-lanes).
- mockup-link-target-text-ok: a link whose target file holds the link's own target-path text reads as ok on origin (origin check, `mockup_on_origin`). Unlikely in normal work: Claude does not write such a file by mistake. Decision: mockup-home (mockups live in the design folder). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- mockup-origin-deleted-branch-ref: an origin ref of a deleted branch that was never pruned still holds the file, so the mockup reads ok after its branch is gone. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-24
- refusal-hedge-word-broad: the hedge words include plain "or" and "if", so "`Bash`: refusal was right (rm -rf or similar)" keeps the row; the block reason does not name the hedge rule (lint/report_guard.py `says_refusal_right`). - multiline-value-edges: in `block_scalar` (lint/workflow_steps.py), a body line indented less than the first is cut mid-text, leading blank lines are dropped, an all-blank `|+` body reads "", and `|2` stays as text. - wait-pid-fixed-pid-flake: the guard test case "wait-pid: a kill -0 loop stays refused" uses the fixed pid 12345. - silent-undo-two-call-sites: lint/check_silent_undo.py is called twice, by the "Silent undo check" run line in checks.yml and by actions/silent-undo/check.sh. The run line stays until bin/ci-job --lint can run a local uses: action, which it reports as unknown. ruling-kind has the same shape: lint/check_ruling_kind.py is called by checks.yml and by actions/ruling-kind/check.sh. Decision: none. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- silent-undo-push-skips-green: with an empty BASE_REF (a push event), actions/silent-undo/check.sh exits 0 with a skip, so the step is green without a check. A caller must gate the step on pull_request, as checks.yml does. Fix: none — action.yml and check.sh already say a run with no pull request base is skipped, and checks.yml gates its call on pull_request. Decision: none.
- precommit-merge-old-offender: during a conflict-resolving merge, `githooks/pre-commit/pre-commit` diffs the index against HEAD. So it refuses a symlink or env file the other branch already committed. Its printed `git rm --cached` would drop that branch's file. Hits only a repo that already tracks an offender. Fix if it fires: when MERGE_HEAD exists, keep only paths that also differ from MERGE_HEAD. Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- hook-bypass-known-limits: rule `hook-bypass` reads one command at a time, so `--config-env=core.hooksPath=VAR`, and `GIT_CONFIG_KEY_<n>` exported in one segment and used in the next, still skip the hooks. Neither is a form Claude writes by mistake. A `git config core.hooksPath` write in one call and the commit in the next also passes, since the write stays in `.git/config`: refuse a lone write of a null path if an incident shows it. A write and a commit inside a later `$( )` text of the same command also pass, as each text is read alone. `rebase`, `am` and `cherry-pick` with `--no-verify` are not covered. Fix if one fires: read an `export` earlier in the same command. Decision: commit-hook-bypass-refused.
- all-jobs-passed-child-key-indent-steps: a child workflow whose `steps:` list sits at the key's indent (valid YAML) reads as 0 steps (`step_blocks`, lint/workflow_steps.py), so `child_problems` (lint/all_jobs_passed_rules.py) says the action step is missing. Red only, never a pass. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-25
- all-jobs-passed-flow-needs-bracket-comment: a multi-line flow `needs: [` with `]` inside a comment on an earlier line cuts the list short (`needs_of`, lint/all_jobs_passed_rules.py) and reports missing jobs. Red only. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- all-jobs-passed-draft-eq-false: an if written `draft == false` or `draft != true` reads as unreadable (`eval_if`, lint/test_windows_split.py), a loud false alarm. A fix moves the cs scope verdicts too. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- all-jobs-passed-job-continue-on-error: a job-level `continue-on-error` on all-jobs-passed or on a needed job is unread by both rule sets, as on main. Claude is unlikely to write it. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- all-jobs-passed-cs-crlf: the cs rules read a CRLF checks.yml as 0 steps. checks.yml is LF; `child_problems` normalizes CRLF. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- builder-glob-past-refusal: in lane all-jobs-passed-wiring a builder met a refusal on a command that named `.github/` and `git` together, and ran its read-only check with the path written `lint/../.gith*/workflows/checks.yml`. guard.log holds no row for it, so the refusal came from outside the repo's guard (the harness's own check), not a named hook here. Briefs now say: stop and report a refusal, never route around it.
- all-jobs-passed-run-both-untested: no case in actions/all-jobs-passed/test_action.sh shows that run.sh still runs wiring.sh after a failed check.sh. A run.sh that stops early still fails the job; only the wiring message is lost until the next run. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- all-jobs-passed-action-path-inline: actions/all-jobs-passed/action.yml runs `bash "${{ github.action_path }}/run.sh"`, the same form main used for check.sh. github.action_path is not caller input. Whether zizmor flags it is unmeasured here; CI runs zizmor on actions/. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- refile-target-unfetched: in `stale_pages.py` batch mode, a not-found page names the claude-settings waiting page as its re-file target even when that page was not fetched in this run (no file, no marker). That page's own line reads `unknown: no fetched file`, so the run still exits 1. Decision: none (skill waiting-page, "Update a repo's pages"). Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26

Fix: allow that form for a session's own unmerged lane branch, or teach lanes to add the trailer before the first push.

Reword to "refuses" when the text is next edited. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26

- verdict-sgr-in-count: EXEC (bin/verdict) reads raw text, so a colour code inside a count line with a marker reads FAIL. Forced colour only. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- playwright-pass-with-no-tests: `npx playwright test --pass-with-no-tests` exits 0 with no count and reads PASS in `verdict`. Opt-in flag only. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26

The line itself is right today. Decision: session-tiers (who started a session decides its merge path) Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
