Rule verification-report-unknown-reads (CLAUDE.md:72) names no guard. No hook checks a report's claims about a record's contents. Found in review of draft PR 2841 (branch model-auth-key-build). Decision: roles-findings-are-debts-or-notes.
- lane-box-parent-covers-triage: The question-box refusal (`hooks/call_judge.py`, `lane_parent`) keys on a parent alone, so triage and the batch runner lose the box. Owner call: are they covered? The machine-load brake (decision `machine-load-brake`, line 14) says a parent alone makes no lane.
- lane-trust-reason-parent-text: `TRUST_REASON` in `hooks/guard_merge.py` says "untagged session" and omits the no-parent condition, and `docs/decisions/session-tiers.md` and `docs/decisions/trust-error-docs-silent.md` name only the cs-build tag. Unknown whether create_session sets a parent on a helper by default (pc-bridge helper workaround).
- lane-defs-differ: Three definitions of a lane disagree: `report_guard` reads tag or parent, the load brake reads tag only, and `call_judge` and `trust_card_hit` read parent only.
- lane-box-mech-cite: `lint/rule_mechanisms.json` cites decision `questions-go-to-owner` for `lane-question-box` and for "an unread record counts as no lane", which that decision does not hold. The claim is true for the box only, since the trust card fails closed.
- lane-box-agent-case-missing: No test covers a box from an `agent_id` call inside a lane, or a session with both a parent and the `cs-build:triage` tag. The remedy `send_message` to `@parent` is one that agents cannot use (decision `agent-no-remote-tools`).
- idle-lanes-reads-closed-lane-item: `idle_lanes` in `skills/roadmap/build.py` may read a lane whose only item is closed (routed) as idle; unpinned.
- routed-waits-skipped: a routed closed item with `waits` is skipped by `kept_answer_lines` before its waits branch.
- routed-kept-answer-at-unchecked: `kept_answer_lines` skips a routed closed item without comparing the kept answer's `at` to `lastAnswer.at`, so a changed answer on it is silent.
- kept-answer-skip-wider-than-routed: `stale_pages.py` `kept_answer_lines` skips a closed item on `lane` and `lastAnswer` alone. `routed_close` in `page_grant.py` also needs a non-blank `home` and a `lastAnswer.at` equal to the answer's `at`. A hand-closed leftover with `lane` and `lastAnswer` but no `home` stays silent in the kept-answer line. Low harm; no code change.
- routed-stalled-row-only-doing-step: the home step's stalled row for a silent routed receiver shows only when the home is a doing or active step whose lane is the receiver (`skills/roadmap/build.py`, laneless). It does not show for a debt home, a done step or a tier receiver. Low harm.
- rule-mechanisms-softening-clause: lint/rule_mechanisms.json marks the whole style-question-form anchor as a guard; its softening-option clause (pick 743 a) is a writing rule only, so the reason text does not say so.
- owner-waits-rejected-act-listed: decision precompact-handoff item 17: an act the owner rejected, or a refusal worked around by a different command, stays unanswered, so a coordinator names it under its owner-waits heading though nothing waits on it. Capped at the newest 5, cleared by one handoff edit. Also: a long first line is shown cut (head and tail) in the block text.
- owner-waits-heading-not-fence-aware: `_OWNER_HEAD.search` in `hooks/precompact_handoff.py` takes the first match of the owner-waits heading, even one quoted inside an earlier fenced block. The section read is then the quoted one, and a coordinator's Stop can block falsely. Found in PR 2685. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- owner-waits-heading-crlf: `_OWNER_HEAD` ends with `[ \t]*$`, which a CRLF heading line does not match, so a handoff saved with CRLF reads as "no section" and the Stop blocks falsely (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Found in PR 2685. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- owner-waits-no-is-error-clears-refusal: `owner_waits_scan` treats a tool result with `is_error` absent as a success, so it clears an earlier refusal of the same act (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Found in PR 2685. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- owner-waits-rstrip-eats-command-dot: `_owner_entries` in `hooks/precompact_handoff.py` strips `.;:,` from the end of each bare line. That also strips punctuation that belongs to a command. A bare line whose command ends in a period (a checkout of the current directory) does not name that command. The backtick form works. Found in PR 2685. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-26
- owner-waits-cap-unpinned: the newest-5 cap in `owner_waits_gap` (`refused[-OWNER_NAMED_MAX:]`, hooks/precompact_handoff.py) has no pinning case: removing the cap turns no case red. Fix: a case with 60 refusals where naming the newest 5 passes and naming 4 blocks. Found in PR 2685.
- owner-waits-empty-section-unpinned: the empty-section branch (`elif not section`, hooks/precompact_handoff.py) has no case: `elif False` turns no case red, though decision precompact-handoff item 17 says an empty section blocks. Fix: a case with the heading and an empty body. Found in PR 2685.
- owner-waits-two-homes: `record_tags` (hooks/own_session.py) repeats the record parse of `_scan`, so one capability has two homes. `_OWNER_HEAD` (hooks/precompact_handoff.py) is a second heading matcher beside the shared one of decision precompact-handoff item 14. A `###` heading or a trailing colon reads as no section, and the block prints the exact heading. Fix: one record reader and one heading matcher, each called from both places. Found in PR 2685.
